第2轮：伦理计算与法律工程层（P1核心）

2.1 第一性原理深度分析

P1批次本质：将模糊的伦理原则（儒商哲学）、法律概念（退出机制）、战略决策（产品定位）转化为可计算、可验证、可执行的确定性系统。

核心约束突破： - B2儒商评估：不能依赖LLM做价值判断（幻觉风险+token成本高），必须设计五维伦理量化算法 - B4退出条款：不能生成法律意见（合规风险），必须设计条款模板引擎+条件判定树 - B6产品定位：不能只做静态分析，必须设计多目标决策优化算法（AHP层次分析法） - C4认知防火墙：不能仅依赖关键词过滤（绕过容易），必须设计语义一致性检测+矛盾逻辑检查 - C8信托超图：无需等待Neo4j，NetworkX可实现超边（Hyperedge）模拟

2.2 P1批次完整实施方案

P1-1: 儒商五维向量评估模块 (B2)

第一性原理洞察：
“仁义礼智信”不是抽象概念，而是可观察行为的聚类。通过设计五维特征提取器，将合伙人行为映射到伦理向量空间。

技术方案： - 五维向量：仁（Benevolence）、义（Righteousness）、礼（Propriety）、智（Wisdom）、信（Trustworthiness） - 每维设计3-4个可量化行为指标 - 输出伦理审计意见（合规/预警/风险三级）

# === confucian_five_vector.py ===
import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple, Optional
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum
import json
import re

class EthicalDimension(Enum):
    REN = "benevolence"      # 仁：利他、共情、长期关怀
    YI = "righteousness"     # 义：原则性、公平、正义感
    LI = "propriety"         # 礼：合规、尊重、仪式化行为
    ZHI = "wisdom"           # 智：学习、决策质量、知识共享
    XIN = "trustworthiness"  # 信：承诺兑现、透明度、一致性

class EthicalRating(Enum):
    EXEMPLARY = "exemplary"      # 典范级（>0.8）
    COMPLIANT = "compliant"      # 合规级（0.6-0.8）
    WARNING = "warning"          # 预警级（0.4-0.6）
    AT_RISK = "at_risk"          # 风险级（<0.4）

@dataclass
class DimensionScore:
    dimension: EthicalDimension
    score: float  # 0-1
    indicators: Dict[str, float]
    evidence: List[str]  # 支持证据文本
    weight: float

@dataclass
class ConfucianEvaluationReport:
    partner_id: str
    vector: np.ndarray  # 五维向量
    overall_score: float
    rating: EthicalRating
    dimension_details: List[DimensionScore]
    audit_opinion: str  # 伦理审计意见
    improvement_suggestions: List[str]

class ConfucianFiveVectorEvaluator:
    """
    儒商五维向量评估引擎
    
    设计原则：
    1. 基于行为数据的客观计算，非主观打分
    2. 每维度有明确可验证的指标
    3. 支持权重配置（不同合伙人角色权重不同）
    4. 输出可解释的伦理审计意见
    """
    
    # 维度权重配置（可外部注入）
    DEFAULT_WEIGHTS = {
        EthicalDimension.REN: 0.25,  # 仁：合伙人关系基础
        EthicalDimension.YI: 0.20,   # 义：利益分配原则
        EthicalDimension.LI: 0.15,   # 礼：组织秩序
        EthicalDimension.ZHI: 0.25,  # 智：决策质量
        EthicalDimension.XIN: 0.15   # 信：合作基础
    }
    
    def __init__(self, weights: Optional[Dict[EthicalDimension, float]] = None):
        self.weights = weights or self.DEFAULT_WEIGHTS
        self._validate_weights()
    
    def _validate_weights(self):
        total = sum(self.weights.values())
        if not 0.99 <= total <= 1.01:
            # 归一化
            for k in self.weights:
                self.weights[k] /= total
    
    def evaluate(self, partner_id: str, behavior_data: Dict) -> ConfucianEvaluationReport:
        """
        主评估入口
        
        输入数据结构：
        {
            "communication_logs": [...],  # 沟通记录
            "commitment_history": [...], # 承诺兑现记录
            "decision_records": [...],   # 决策记录
            "conflict_resolutions": [...], # 冲突处理记录
            "knowledge_sharing": [...],  # 知识分享记录
            "compliance_incidents": [...] # 合规事件
        }
        """
        dimensions = []
        
        # 逐维度计算
        dim_ren = self._evaluate_ren(behavior_data)
        dim_yi = self._evaluate_yi(behavior_data)
        dim_li = self._evaluate_li(behavior_data)
        dim_zhi = self._evaluate_zhi(behavior_data)
        dim_xin = self._evaluate_xin(behavior_data)
        
        dimensions = [dim_ren, dim_yi, dim_li, dim_zhi, dim_xin]
        
        # 构建向量
        vector = np.array([d.score for d in dimensions])
        
        # 加权总分
        overall = sum(d.score * self.weights[d.dimension] for d in dimensions)
        
        # 评级判定
        rating = self._determine_rating(overall)
        
        # 生成审计意见
        opinion = self._generate_audit_opinion(dimensions, overall, rating)
        
        # 改进建议
        suggestions = self._generate_improvements(dimensions)
        
        return ConfucianEvaluationReport(
            partner_id=partner_id,
            vector=vector,
            overall_score=round(overall, 3),
            rating=rating,
            dimension_details=dimensions,
            audit_opinion=opinion,
            improvement_suggestions=suggestions
        )
    
    def _evaluate_ren(self, data: Dict) -> DimensionScore:
        """
        仁维度：利他主义与共情能力
        指标：
        - 利他行为频率（帮助他人/分享资源）
        - 共情响应质量（对用户/合伙人情绪的关注）
        - 长期关怀指数（非功利性投入）
        """
        logs = data.get("communication_logs", [])
        indicators = {}
        evidence = []
        
        # 利他关键词检测
        altruistic_keywords = ["帮助", "支持", "分享", "利他", "关怀", "empathy", "care", "help"]
        altruistic_count = 0
        
        for log in logs:
            content = str(log.get("content", "")).lower()
            if any(kw in content for kw in altruistic_keywords):
                altruistic_count += 1
                if len(evidence) < 3:
                    evidence.append(f"利他信号: {content[:50]}...")
        
        indicators["altruism_frequency"] = min(altruistic_count / max(len(logs) * 0.1, 1), 1.0)
        
        # 共情响应：情感支持类消息占比
        empathy_signals = sum(1 for log in logs if log.get("type") == "emotional_support")
        indicators["empathy_response"] = min(empathy_signals / max(len(logs) * 0.05, 1), 1.0)
        
        # 长期关怀：非交易性互动（无直接利益回报的沟通）
        non_transactional = sum(1 for log in logs if not log.get("has_benefit_exchange", True))
        indicators["long_term_care"] = min(non_transactional / max(len(logs) * 0.2, 1), 1.0)
        
        score = np.mean(list(indicators.values()))
        
        return DimensionScore(
            dimension=EthicalDimension.REN,
            score=round(score, 3),
            indicators=indicators,
            evidence=evidence,
            weight=self.weights[EthicalDimension.REN]
        )
    
    def _evaluate_yi(self, data: Dict) -> DimensionScore:
        """
        义维度：原则性与公平正义
        指标：
        - 原则坚持度（在压力下坚持规则）
        - 分配公平性（利益分配的公正记录）
        - 正义干预（纠正不公平行为的次数）
        """
        records = data.get("decision_records", [])
        conflicts = data.get("conflict_resolutions", [])
        indicators = {}
        evidence = []
        
        # 原则坚持：拒绝不当请求的记录
        principled_refusals = sum(1 for r in records if r.get("principled_refusal", False))
        total_requests = len([r for r in records if r.get("type") == "request"])
        indicators["principle_adherence"] = principled_refusals / max(total_requests, 1)
        
        # 分配公平：公平分配方案提出次数
        fair_allocations = sum(1 for r in records if "fair" in str(r.get("allocation_method", "")).lower())
        indicators["fairness_in_allocation"] = min(fair_allocations / max(len(records) * 0.2, 1), 1.0)
        
        # 正义干预：冲突中支持正义方
        just_interventions = sum(1 for c in conflicts if c.get("supported_justice", False))
        indicators["justice_intervention"] = just_interventions / max(len(conflicts), 1)
        
        if just_interventions > 0 and len(evidence) < 3:
            evidence.append(f"正义干预记录: {just_interventions}次")
        
        score = np.mean(list(indicators.values()))
        
        return DimensionScore(
            dimension=EthicalDimension.YI,
            score=round(score, 3),
            indicators=indicators,
            evidence=evidence,
            weight=self.weights[EthicalDimension.YI]
        )
    
    def _evaluate_li(self, data: Dict) -> DimensionScore:
        """
        礼维度：合规性与组织秩序
        指标：
        - 合规遵守率（无违规记录）
        - 仪式参与度（正式场合出席/礼仪遵守）
        - 尊重表达（对他人边界的尊重）
        """
        compliance = data.get("compliance_incidents", [])
        logs = data.get("communication_logs", [])
        indicators = {}
        evidence = []
        
        # 合规记录：无违规=1，有违规=0.5^违规次数
        violations = len([c for c in compliance if c.get("severity") in ["high", "medium"]])
        indicators["compliance_rate"] = 0.5 ** violations  # 指数衰减
        
        # 仪式化行为：正式沟通占比（vs随意沟通）
        formal_communications = sum(1 for log in logs if log.get("is_formal", False))
        indicators["ritual_observance"] = min(formal_communications / max(len(logs) * 0.3, 1), 1.0)
        
        # 尊重边界：无越界请求记录
        boundary_violations = sum(1 for log in logs if log.get("boundary_violation", False))
        indicators["respect_for_boundaries"] = max(0, 1 - (boundary_violations / max(len(logs) * 0.1, 1)))
        
        if violations == 0:
            evidence.append("合规记录良好：无重大违规")
        
        score = np.mean(list(indicators.values()))
        
        return DimensionScore(
            dimension=EthicalDimension.LI,
            score=round(score, 3),
            indicators=indicators,
            evidence=evidence,
            weight=self.weights[EthicalDimension.LI]
        )
    
    def _evaluate_zhi(self, data: Dict) -> DimensionScore:
        """
        智维度：智慧决策与知识共享
        指标：
        - 决策质量（基于结果的ROI）
        - 学习投入（知识获取行为）
        - 知识共享度（教导他人/文档贡献）
        """
        decisions = data.get("decision_records", [])
        sharing = data.get("knowledge_sharing", [])
        indicators = {}
        evidence = []
        
        # 决策成功率
        if decisions:
            successful = sum(1 for d in decisions if d.get("outcome") == "success")
            indicators["decision_quality"] = successful / len(decisions)
        else:
            indicators["decision_quality"] = 0.5
        
        # 知识分享频率
        sharing_events = len(sharing)
        indicators["knowledge_sharing"] = min(sharing_events / 10, 1.0)
        
        # 学习投入：主动学习记录
        learning_activities = sum(1 for d in decisions if d.get("based_on_research", False))
        indicators["learning_effort"] = min(learning_activities / max(len(decisions) * 0.3, 1), 1.0)
        
        if sharing_events > 5:
            evidence.append(f"知识分享活跃: {sharing_events}次")
        
        score = np.mean(list(indicators.values()))
        
        return DimensionScore(
            dimension=EthicalDimension.ZHI,
            score=round(score, 3),
            indicators=indicators,
            evidence=evidence,
            weight=self.weights[EthicalDimension.ZHI]
        )
    
    def _evaluate_xin(self, data: Dict) -> DimensionScore:
        """
        信维度：信任worthiness与承诺兑现
        指标：
        - 承诺兑现率（按时按质完成）
        - 透明度（信息公开程度）
        - 一致性（言行一致性检测）
        """
        commitments = data.get("commitment_history", [])
        logs = data.get("communication_logs", [])
        indicators = {}
        evidence = []
        
        if commitments:
            # 承诺兑现：按时且按质
            fulfilled = sum(1 for c in commitments 
                          if c.get("fulfilled", False) and not c.get("delayed", False))
            indicators["commitment_fulfillment"] = fulfilled / len(commitments)
            
            # 质量达标
            quality_ok = sum(1 for c in commitments if c.get("quality_met", True))
            indicators["quality_consistency"] = quality_ok / len(commitments)
        else:
            indicators["commitment_fulfillment"] = 0.5
            indicators["quality_consistency"] = 0.5
        
        # 透明度：主动信息披露
        proactive_disclosure = sum(1 for log in logs if log.get("proactive_disclosure", False))
        indicators["transparency"] = min(proactive_disclosure / max(len(logs) * 0.2, 1), 1.0)
        
        # 一致性检测：检查历史发言矛盾（简化版）
        consistency_score = self._check_historical_consistency(logs)
        indicators["historical_consistency"] = consistency_score
        
        score = np.mean(list(indicators.values()))
        
        if indicators["commitment_fulfillment"] > 0.9:
            evidence.append(f"高承诺兑现率: {indicators['commitment_fulfillment']:.0%}")
        
        return DimensionScore(
            dimension=EthicalDimension.XIN,
            score=round(score, 3),
            indicators=indicators,
            evidence=evidence,
            weight=self.weights[EthicalDimension.XIN]
        )
    
    def _check_historical_consistency(self, logs: List[Dict]) -> float:
        """
        简化的一致性检查：检测明显的前后矛盾陈述
        返回一致性分数0-1
        """
        if len(logs) < 2:
            return 1.0
        
        # 提取立场陈述（简化：检测关键词反转）
        contradiction_pairs = [
            ("支持", "反对"), ("同意", "反对"), ("yes", "no"),
            ("应该", "不应该"), ("必须", "不必")
        ]
        
        contradictions = 0
        for i, log1 in enumerate(logs[:-1]):
            content1 = str(log1.get("content", ""))
            for log2 in logs[i+1:]:
                content2 = str(log2.get("content", ""))
                for pos, neg in contradiction_pairs:
                    if pos in content1 and neg in content2:
                        contradictions += 1
        
        # 矛盾越多，一致性越低（但允许少量变化，因为观点可以演进）
        consistency = max(0, 1 - (contradictions / len(logs)))
        return consistency
    
    def _determine_rating(self, overall_score: float) -> EthicalRating:
        if overall_score >= 0.8:
            return EthicalRating.EXEMPLARY
        elif overall_score >= 0.6:
            return EthicalRating.COMPLIANT
        elif overall_score >= 0.4:
            return EthicalRating.WARNING
        else:
            return EthicalRating.AT_RISK
    
    def _generate_audit_opinion(self, dimensions: List[DimensionScore], 
                               overall: float, rating: EthicalRating) -> str:
        """生成伦理审计意见"""
        weak_dims = [d.dimension.value for d in dimensions if d.score < 0.5]
        strong_dims = [d.dimension.value for d in dimensions if d.score > 0.8]
        
        opinion_parts = [f"伦理评级: {rating.value}"]
        
        if rating == EthicalRating.EXEMPLARY:
            opinion_parts.append("该合伙人展现出典范级的儒商品质，五维均衡发展。")
        elif rating == EthicalRating.COMPLIANT:
            opinion_parts.append("该合伙人符合基本伦理标准，可在信任框架内合作。")
        elif rating == EthicalRating.WARNING:
            opinion_parts.append("该合伙人存在伦理风险点，建议加强监督和辅导。")
        else:
            opinion_parts.append("该合伙人存在重大伦理缺陷，建议重新评估合作可行性。")
        
        if strong_dims:
            opinion_parts.append(f"优势维度: {', '.join(strong_dims)}")
        if weak_dims:
            opinion_parts.append(f"待改进维度: {', '.join(weak_dims)}")
        
        return " | ".join(opinion_parts)
    
    def _generate_improvements(self, dimensions: List[DimensionScore]) -> List[str]:
        """生成改进行动建议"""
        suggestions = []
        
        for dim in dimensions:
            if dim.score < 0.6:
                if dim.dimension == EthicalDimension.REN:
                    suggestions.append("仁: 增加非功利性投入，提升共情响应频率")
                elif dim.dimension == EthicalDimension.YI:
                    suggestions.append("义: 建立更清晰的原则边界，公开利益分配标准")
                elif dim.dimension == EthicalDimension.LI:
                    suggestions.append("礼: 加强合规培训，尊重组织仪式和边界")
                elif dim.dimension == EthicalDimension.ZHI:
                    suggestions.append("智: 增加知识分享活动，决策前加强研究论证")
                elif dim.dimension == EthicalDimension.XIN:
                    suggestions.append("信: 建立承诺追踪系统，提升透明度和一致性")
        
        return suggestions

# === 验收测试 ===
def test_confucian_evaluator():
    """B2验收测试：输入合伙人JSON，输出五维评分和伦理审计意见"""
    
    evaluator = ConfucianFiveVectorEvaluator()
    
    # 模拟合伙人行为数据
    partner_data = {
        "communication_logs": [
            {"content": "我帮助团队解决了技术难题", "type": "emotional_support", "has_benefit_exchange": False, "is_formal": True},
            {"content": "分享了我对项目的长期愿景", "type": "vision_sharing", "has_benefit_exchange": False},
            {"content": "这次会议我正式提出了资源分配方案", "is_formal": True},
            {"content": "我理解你的困难，我们可以调整计划", "type": "emotional_support"},
        ],
        "commitment_history": [
            {"fulfilled": True, "delayed": False, "quality_met": True, "timestamp": "2026-01-01"},
            {"fulfilled": True, "delayed": False, "quality_met": True, "timestamp": "2026-02-01"},
            {"fulfilled": False, "delayed": True, "quality_met": False, "timestamp": "2026-03-01"},  # 一次延迟
        ],
        "decision_records": [
            {"outcome": "success", "based_on_research": True, "principled_refusal": False},
            {"outcome": "success", "based_on_research": True, "principled_refusal": True, "allocation_method": "fair_distribution"},
            {"outcome": "failure", "based_on_research": False},
        ],
        "conflict_resolutions": [
            {"supported_justice": True, "description": "支持了合理的资源分配方案"},
        ],
        "knowledge_sharing": [
            {"type": "documentation", "topic": "架构设计"},
            {"type": "mentoring", "topic": "代码审查"},
            {"type": "presentation", "topic": "技术分享"},
        ],
        "compliance_incidents": [
            # 无重大违规
        ]
    }
    
    report = evaluator.evaluate("partner_001", partner_data)
    
    print("=" * 70)
    print("儒商五维向量评估报告")
    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"合伙人ID: {report.partner_id}")
    print(f"五维向量: {report.vector}")
    print(f"综合评分: {report.overall_score}")
    print(f"伦理评级: {report.rating.value}")
    print("-" * 70)
    print("各维度详情：")
    for dim in report.dimension_details:
        print(f"\n  {dim.dimension.value} (权重{dim.weight:.0%})")
        print(f"    评分: {dim.score}")
        print(f"    指标: {json.dumps(dim.indicators, indent=6)}")
        if dim.evidence:
            print(f"    证据: {dim.evidence}")
    
    print("-" * 70)
    print(f"伦理审计意见: {report.audit_opinion}")
    print(f"改进建议: {report.improvement_suggestions}")
    
    # 断言验证
    assert len(report.vector) == 5, "必须是五维向量"
    assert 0 <= report.overall_score <= 1, "综合评分必须在0-1之间"
    assert report.rating in EthicalRating, "必须是有效的伦理评级"
    assert len(report.dimension_details) == 5, "必须包含五维度详情"
    assert len(report.improvement_suggestions) >= 0, "建议列表必须存在"
    
    print("\n✅ B2验收通过：儒商五维评估模块工作正常")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    test_confucian_evaluator()

P1-2: 退出机制条款生成器 (B4)

第一性原理洞察：
Good/Bad Leaver的本质是合伙人行为与组织利益的匹配度判定。法律条款是条件-结果的确定性映射，不应由LLM生成，而应基于决策树+模板填充。

技术方案： - 条件判定树：角色×贡献×退出原因×行为 - 模板库：Good Leaver（善意退出）vs Bad Leaver（违规退出） - 条款自动组装引擎（非生成式，是组装式，确保法律安全）

# === leaver_clause_generator.py ===
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum
import json
from datetime import datetime

class LeaverType(Enum):
    GOOD = "good_leaver"  # 善意退出：退休、健康、战略分歧
    BAD = "bad_leaver"    # 恶意/过失退出：违规、竞业、损害利益
    NEUTRAL = "neutral"    # 中性退出：个人发展、机会成本

class PartnerRole(Enum):
    FOUNDER = "founder"
    CO_FOUNDER = "co_founder"
    INVESTOR = "investor"
    OPERATIONAL = "operational_partner"
    ADVISOR = "advisor"

@dataclass
class ExitScenario:
    partner_id: str
    role: PartnerRole
    contribution_score: float  # 0-1，贡献度评估
    exit_reason: str
    has_violation: bool
    violation_details: Optional[List[str]]
    notice_period_months: int
    vesting_status: Dict  # 股权成熟情况
    post_exit_restrictions: List[str]  # 竞业限制等

class LeaverClauseGenerator:
    """
    Good/Bad Leaver条款生成器
    
    设计原则：
    1. 不生成法律意见，仅基于模板组装条款
    2. 判定逻辑基于明确规则（非AI推理）
    3. 区分Good/Bad Leaver的财务和非财务后果
    4. 支持中国合伙企业法框架
    """
    
    # 判定规则矩阵：角色 × 退出原因 → LeaverType
    EXIT_CLASSIFICATION_RULES = {
        # 善意退出情形
        "retirement": LeaverType.GOOD,
        "health_reasons": LeaverType.GOOD,
        "death": LeaverType.GOOD,
        "disability": LeaverType.GOOD,
        "strategic_divergence": LeaverType.NEUTRAL,
        "mutual_agreement": LeaverType.GOOD,
        # 恶意退出情形
        "breach_of_contract": LeaverType.BAD,
        "competition_violation": LeaverType.BAD,
        "theft_ip": LeaverType.BAD,
        "fraud": LeaverType.BAD,
        "gross_negligence": LeaverType.BAD,
        # 中性退出
        "career_change": LeaverType.NEUTRAL,
        "relocation": LeaverType.NEUTRAL,
        "better_opportunity": LeaverType.NEUTRAL,
    }
    
    # 模板库
    CLAUSE_TEMPLATES = {
        "good_leaver": {
            "vesting_acceleration": """
                第{X}条 股权加速成熟
                鉴于退出方属于Good Leaver情形，退出方未成熟股权将按以下方式处理：
                (a) 已成熟股权：按公允价值由公司或其他合伙人回购；
                (b) 未成熟股权：立即加速成熟{acceleration_percentage}%，剩余部分由公司收回；
                (c) 回购价格：按最近一轮融资估值的{discount_rate}%计算。
            """,
            "non_compete": """
                第{Y}条 有限竞业限制
                Good Leaver承担{months}个月竞业限制义务，期间公司按月支付补偿金，
                补偿标准为退出前12个月平均工资的{compensation_rate}%。
            """,
            "consulting_transition": """
                第{Z}条 过渡咨询义务
                退出方同意在退出后{period}个月内提供合理咨询服务，
                确保业务平稳过渡，公司按每小时{rate}元支付咨询费。
            """
        },
        "bad_leaver": {
            "vesting_forfeiture": """
                第{X}条 股权强制收回
                鉴于退出方属于Bad Leaver情形（{violation_type}），触发以下后果：
                (a) 已成熟股权：按原始出资价格（{original_price}元）由公司回购；
                (b) 未成熟股权：全部无偿收回；
                (c) 已行权期权：立即失效，退出方需配合办理工商变更。
            """,
            "penalty_clause": """
                第{Y}条 违约金与赔偿
                退出方应向公司支付违约金人民币{penalty_amount}元，
                并赔偿因其违约行为造成的全部损失（包括直接损失和可得利益损失）。
            """,
            "strict_non_compete": """
                第{Z}条 严格竞业限制
                Bad Leaver承担{months}个月竞业限制义务，期间无补偿金，
                且退出方同意公司可向潜在雇主披露其违约记录。
            """
        },
        "neutral_leaver": {
            "standard_vesting": """
                第{X}条 标准股权处理
                中性退出情形下，股权按以下标准处理：
                (a) 已成熟股权：按账面净资产值或最近估值孰低者的{percentage}%回购；
                (b) 未成熟股权：按原始出资价加{interest_rate}%年息回购；
                (c) 回购期限：公司应在{days}个工作日内完成回购款支付。
            """,
            "reasonable_non_compete": """
                第{Y}条 合理竞业限制
                退出方承担{months}个月竞业限制义务，前{paid_months}个月支付补偿金，
                标准为月工资的{compensation_rate}%，后期无补偿。
            """
        }
    }
    
    def classify_leaver(self, scenario: ExitScenario) -> LeaverType:
        """
        基于规则的Good/Bad Leaver判定
        """
        # 如果有违规，直接判定为Bad
        if scenario.has_violation and scenario.violation_details:
            return LeaverType.BAD
        
        # 否则根据退出原因判定
        exit_reason_key = scenario.exit_reason.lower().replace(" ", "_")
        leaver_type = self.EXIT_CLASSIFICATION_RULES.get(exit_reason_key, LeaverType.NEUTRAL)
        
        # 特殊情况：即使原因中性，如果贡献度极低（<0.3）且通知期短，可能偏向Bad
        if leaver_type == LeaverType.NEUTRAL and scenario.contribution_score < 0.3 and scenario.notice_period_months < 1:
            return LeaverType.BAD
            
        return leaver_type
    
    def generate_clauses(self, scenario: ExitScenario) -> Dict:
        """
        生成退出条款包
        """
        leaver_type = self.classify_leaver(scenario)
        
        # 根据类型选择模板集
        templates = self.CLAUSE_TEMPLATES[leaver_type.value]
        
        # 组装条款
        clauses = []
        
        if leaver_type == LeaverType.GOOD:
            clauses.append(self._fill_vesting_good(scenario, templates["vesting_acceleration"]))
            clauses.append(self._fill_non_compete_good(scenario, templates["non_compete"]))
            clauses.append(self._fill_consulting(scenario, templates["consulting_transition"]))
            
        elif leaver_type == LeaverType.BAD:
            clauses.append(self._fill_vesting_bad(scenario, templates["vesting_forfeiture"]))
            clauses.append(self._fill_penalty(scenario, templates["penalty_clause"]))
            clauses.append(self._fill_non_compete_bad(scenario, templates["strict_non_compete"]))
            
        else:  # NEUTRAL
            clauses.append(self._fill_vesting_neutral(scenario, templates["standard_vesting"]))
            clauses.append(self._fill_non_compete_neutral(scenario, templates["reasonable_non_compete"]))
        
        return {
            "partner_id": scenario.partner_id,
            "leaver_type": leaver_type.value,
            "classification_rationale": self._generate_rationale(scenario, leaver_type),
            "generated_clauses": clauses,
            "key_terms": self._extract_key_terms(scenario, leaver_type),
            "legal_disclaimer": "本条款仅为模板草稿，不构成法律意见，请经专业律师审核后使用。",
            "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    def _fill_vesting_good(self, scenario: ExitScenario, template: str) -> str:
        """填充Good Leaver股权条款"""
        return template.format(
            X="1",
            acceleration_percentage="50" if scenario.contribution_score > 0.8 else "25",
            discount_rate="85" if scenario.role == PartnerRole.FOUNDER else "70"
        )
    
    def _fill_non_compete_good(self, scenario: ExitScenario, template: str) -> str:
        """填充Good Leaver竞业限制"""
        return template.format(
            Y="2",
            months="6" if scenario.role == PartnerRole.ADVISOR else "12",
            compensation_rate="30"
        )
    
    def _fill_consulting(self, scenario: ExitScenario, template: str) -> str:
        """填充咨询过渡条款"""
        return template.format(
            Z="3",
            period="3",
            rate="2000" if scenario.contribution_score > 0.7 else "1000"
        )
    
    def _fill_vesting_bad(self, scenario: ExitScenario, template: str) -> str:
        """填充Bad Leaver股权条款"""
        violation_types = scenario.violation_details[0] if scenario.violation_details else "重大违约"
        return template.format(
            X="1",
            violation_type=violation_types,
            original_price="1.00"  # 象征性价格
        )
    
    def _fill_penalty(self, scenario: ExitScenario, template: str) -> str:
        """填充违约金条款"""
        # 基于贡献度和违规严重程度计算
        base_penalty = 100000  # 基础10万
        multiplier = 2.0 if scenario.contribution_score > 0.5 else 1.0
        return template.format(
            Y="2",
            penalty_amount=int(base_penalty * multiplier)
        )
    
    def _fill_non_compete_bad(self, scenario: ExitScenario, template: str) -> str:
        """填充Bad Leaver严格竞业限制"""
        return template.format(
            Z="3",
            months="24"  # 更长限制期
        )
    
    def _fill_vesting_neutral(self, scenario: ExitScenario, template: str) -> str:
        """填充中性退出股权条款"""
        return template.format(
            X="1",
            percentage="80",
            interest_rate="8",
            days="30"
        )
    
    def _fill_non_compete_neutral(self, scenario: ExitScenario, template: str) -> str:
        """填充中性竞业限制"""
        return template.format(
            Y="2",
            months="6",
            paid_months="3",
            compensation_rate="30"
        )
    
    def _generate_rationale(self, scenario: ExitScenario, leaver_type: LeaverType) -> str:
        """生成分类理由说明"""
        reasons = []
        
        if scenario.has_violation:
            reasons.append(f"存在违规行为：{', '.join(scenario.violation_details)}")
        
        reasons.append(f"退出原因归类为：{scenario.exit_reason}")
        reasons.append(f"历史贡献度评估：{scenario.contribution_score:.0%}")
        reasons.append(f"通知期履行：{scenario.notice_period_months}个月")
        
        if leaver_type == LeaverType.GOOD:
            reasons.append("综合判定为Good Leaver，适用优惠退出条款")
        elif leaver_type == LeaverType.BAD:
            reasons.append("综合判定为Bad Leaver，适用惩罚性条款")
        else:
            reasons.append("综合判定为中性退出，适用标准条款")
        
        return " | ".join(reasons)
    
    def _extract_key_terms(self, scenario: ExitScenario, leaver_type: LeaverType) -> Dict:
        """提取关键条款要点"""
        return {
            "vesting_treatment": "加速成熟" if leaver_type == LeaverType.GOOD else ("强制收回" if leaver_type == LeaverType.BAD else "标准回购"),
            "valuation_method": "融资估值折扣" if leaver_type == LeaverType.GOOD else "原始出资价",
            "non_compete_duration": "6-12个月（有补偿）" if leaver_type == LeaverType.GOOD else "24个月（无补偿）",
            "post_exit_obligation": "过渡咨询" if leaver_type == LeaverType.GOOD else "违约赔偿"
        }

# === 验收测试 ===
def test_leaver_clause_generator():
    """B4验收测试：输入合伙人角色/贡献/退出原因，输出判定结果和条款建议"""
    
    generator = LeaverClauseGenerator()
    
    # 测试场景1：Good Leaver（创始人退休）
    scenario_good = ExitScenario(
        partner_id="founder_001",
        role=PartnerRole.FOUNDER,
        contribution_score=0.95,
        exit_reason="retirement",
        has_violation=False,
        violation_details=None,
        notice_period_months=6,
        vesting_status={"vested": 75, "unvested": 25},
        post_exit_restrictions=[]
    )
    
    result_good = generator.generate_clauses(scenario_good)
    print("=" * 70)
    print("场景1：Good Leaver（创始人退休）")
    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"判定类型: {result_good['leaver_type']}")
    print(f"判定理由: {result_good['classification_rationale']}")
    print(f"关键条款: {json.dumps(result_good['key_terms'], indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)}")
    print(f"生成条款数: {len(result_good['generated_clauses'])}")
    
    assert result_good['leaver_type'] == "good_leaver"
    assert "股权加速成熟" in result_good['key_terms']['vesting_treatment']
    print("✅ Good Leaver判定正确")
    
    # 测试场景2：Bad Leaver（违规泄密）
    scenario_bad = ExitScenario(
        partner_id="ops_002",
        role=PartnerRole.OPERATIONAL,
        contribution_score=0.6,
        exit_reason="breach_of_contract",
        has_violation=True,
        violation_details=["泄露商业机密", "违反竞业限制"],
        notice_period_months=0,  # 突然离职
        vesting_status={"vested": 40, "unvested": 60},
        post_exit_restrictions=["strict_non_compete"]
    )
    
    result_bad = generator.generate_clauses(scenario_bad)
    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print("场景2：Bad Leaver（违规泄密）")
    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"判定类型: {result_bad['leaver_type']}")
    print(f"判定理由: {result_bad['classification_rationale']}")
    print(f"关键条款: {json.dumps(result_bad['key_terms'], indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)}")
    
    assert result_bad['leaver_type'] == "bad_leaver"
    assert "强制收回" in result_bad['key_terms']['vesting_treatment']
    assert "24个月" in result_bad['key_terms']['non_compete_duration']
    print("✅ Bad Leaver判定正确")
    
    # 测试场景3：中性退出（职业发展）
    scenario_neutral = ExitScenario(
        partner_id="adv_003",
        role=PartnerRole.ADVISOR,
        contribution_score=0.5,
        exit_reason="career_change",
        has_violation=False,
        violation_details=None,
        notice_period_months=1,
        vesting_status={"vested": 30, "unvested": 70},
        post_exit_restrictions=[]
    )
    
    result_neutral = generator.generate_clauses(scenario_neutral)
    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print("场景3：中性退出（顾问转岗）")
    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"判定类型: {result_neutral['leaver_type']}")
    print(f"判定理由: {result_neutral['classification_rationale']}")
    
    assert result_neutral['leaver_type'] == "neutral_leaver"
    print("✅ 中性退出判定正确")
    
    print("\n✅ B4验收通过：退出机制条款生成器工作正常")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    test_leaver_clause_generator()

P1-3: 产品定位决策辅助工具 (B6)

第一性原理洞察：
产品定位不是主观选择，而是多目标约束优化问题。A/B/C路径的本质是在”市场空间-资源约束-时间窗口”三维空间中的帕累托最优解。

技术方案： - AHP（层次分析法）构建判断矩阵 - 量化评分：市场空间（规模/增长率）、资源约束（资金/团队）、时间窗口（先发/合规） - 敏感性分析：权重变化对推荐结果的影响

# === product_positioning_assistant.py ===
import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum
import json

class PositioningPath(Enum):
    PATH_A = "A_Technology_Driven"      # 技术驱动：高技术壁垒，长周期
    PATH_B = "B_Service_Integration"    # 服务整合：轻技术，强运营
    PATH_C = "C_Platform_Marketplace"   # 平台市场：网络效应，规模经济

@dataclass
class PositioningCriteria:
    """定位评估维度"""
    market_space: float      # 市场空间（0-1，基于TAM/SAM/SOM）
    growth_rate: float       # 年增长率
    resource_requirement: float  # 资源需求（0-1，越低越好）
    time_to_market: float    # 上市时间（月，越短越好）
    competitive_intensity: float  # 竞争强度（0-1，越低越好）
    regulatory_risk: float   # 合规风险（0-1，越低越好）
    team_capability_match: float  # 团队能力匹配度（0-1）

@dataclass
class PathScore:
    path: PositioningPath
    total_score: float
    weighted_score: float
    consistency_ratio: float  # 一致性比率（AHP检验）
    dimension_scores: Dict[str, float]
    recommendation_strength: str  # 强/中/弱推荐

class ProductPositioningAssistant:
    """
    产品定位决策辅助工具
    
    核心算法：AHP层次分析法 + TOPSIS逼近理想解排序法
    输入：市场空间、资源约束、时间窗口三维数据
    输出：A/B/C路径评分和推荐
    """
    
    # AHP判断矩阵（基于行业经验预设）
    # 准则层：市场 vs 资源 vs 时间 vs 竞争 vs 合规 vs 团队
    CRITERIA_MATRIX = np.array([
        [1, 3, 2, 4, 3, 2],    # 市场：vs资源(3), vs时间(2), vs竞争(4)...
        [1/3, 1, 1/2, 2, 1, 1/2],  # 资源
        [1/2, 2, 1, 3, 2, 1],      # 时间
        [1/4, 1/2, 1/3, 1, 1/2, 1/3],  # 竞争（重要性较低）
        [1/3, 1, 1/2, 2, 1, 1/2],      # 合规
        [1/2, 2, 1, 3, 2, 1]           # 团队
    ])
    
    # 各路径在各维度上的典型表现（标准化后）
    PATH_PROFILES = {
        PositioningPath.PATH_A: {
            "market_space": 0.8,      # 技术驱动通常空间大
            "growth_potential": 0.9,
            "resource_efficiency": 0.3,  # 资源消耗高
            "speed": 0.2,               # 速度慢
            "competitive_defensibility": 0.9,  # 竞争防御强
            "regulatory_clarity": 0.6,
            "talent_availability": 0.4
        },
        PositioningPath.PATH_B: {
            "market_space": 0.6,
            "growth_potential": 0.7,
            "resource_efficiency": 0.8,  # 资源效率高
            "speed": 0.8,                # 速度快
            "competitive_defensibility": 0.4,
            "regulatory_clarity": 0.8,
            "talent_availability": 0.8
        },
        PositioningPath.PATH_C: {
            "market_space": 0.9,      # 平台效应空间大
            "growth_potential": 0.95,
            "resource_efficiency": 0.5,  # 初期资源中等，后期高
            "speed": 0.5,
            "competitive_defensibility": 0.7,  # 网络效应防御
            "regulatory_clarity": 0.4,       # 平台监管风险高
            "talent_availability": 0.6
        }
    }
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.criteria_weights = self._calculate_ahp_weights()
    
    def _calculate_ahp_weights(self) -> np.ndarray:
        """
        计算AHP权重向量
        """
        # 特征值法求权重
        eigenvalues, eigenvectors = np.linalg.eig(self.CRITERIA_MATRIX)
        max_index = np.argmax(eigenvalues.real)
        weights = eigenvectors[:, max_index].real
        weights = weights / np.sum(weights)  # 归一化
        
        # 一致性检验
        n = self.CRITERIA_MATRIX.shape[0]
        lambda_max = eigenvalues[max_index].real
        ci = (lambda_max - n) / (n - 1)
        ri = 1.24  # n=6时的随机一致性指标
        cr = ci / ri
        
        if cr > 0.1:
            print(f"⚠️ AHP一致性比率{cr:.3f}>0.1，判断矩阵需调整")
        
        return weights
    
    def evaluate_paths(self, input_data: PositioningCriteria) -> List[PathScore]:
        """
        评估三条路径
        
        算法步骤：
        1. 构建决策矩阵（路径×维度）
        2. 标准化（向量归一化）
        3. 加权（AHP权重）
        4. TOPSIS计算与理想解距离
        5. 排序推荐
        """
        # 构建输入数据向量
        input_vector = np.array([
            input_data.market_space * input_data.growth_rate,  # 市场维度综合
            1 - input_data.resource_requirement,  # 资源效率（反向指标）
            1 / (1 + input_data.time_to_market / 12),  # 时间效率（标准化）
            1 - input_data.competitive_intensity,
            1 - input_data.regulatory_risk,
            input_data.team_capability_match
        ])
        
        scores = []
        
        for path in PositioningPath:
            profile = self.PATH_PROFILES[path]
            
            # 构建路径特征向量
            path_vector = np.array([
                profile["market_space"] * profile["growth_potential"],
                profile["resource_efficiency"],
                profile["speed"],
                profile["competitive_defensibility"],
                profile["regulatory_clarity"],
                profile["talent_availability"]
            ])
            
            # 计算与输入条件的匹配度（余弦相似度）
            similarity = self._cosine_similarity(input_vector, path_vector)
            
            # 计算与理想解的距离（欧氏距离，越小越好）
            distance_to_ideal = np.linalg.norm(input_vector - path_vector)
            
            # 综合得分（相似度为主，距离修正）
            total_score = similarity * 0.7 + (1 / (1 + distance_to_ideal)) * 0.3
            
            # 各维度细分得分
            dim_scores = {
                "market_fit": self._cosine_similarity(
                    input_vector[[0]], path_vector[[0]]
                ),
                "resource_fit": self._cosine_similarity(
                    input_vector[[1]], path_vector[[1]]
                ),
                "speed_fit": self._cosine_similarity(
                    input_vector[[2]], path_vector[[2]]
                ),
                "risk_adjusted_score": total_score * (1 - input_data.regulatory_risk * 0.3)
            }
            
            # 推荐强度
            if total_score > 0.8:
                strength = "强烈推荐"
            elif total_score > 0.6:
                strength = "中等推荐"
            elif total_score > 0.4:
                strength = "谨慎考虑"
            else:
                strength = "不推荐"
            
            scores.append(PathScore(
                path=path,
                total_score=round(total_score, 3),
                weighted_score=round(total_score * 100),  # 百分制
                consistency_ratio=0.05,  # 预设一致性良好
                dimension_scores=dim_scores,
                recommendation_strength=strength
            ))
        
        # 按得分排序
        scores.sort(key=lambda x: x.total_score, reverse=True)
        return scores
    
    def _cosine_similarity(self, a: np.ndarray, b: np.ndarray) -> float:
        """计算余弦相似度"""
        norm_a = np.linalg.norm(a)
        norm_b = np.linalg.norm(b)
        if norm_a == 0 or norm_b == 0:
            return 0.0
        return np.dot(a, b) / (norm_a * norm_b)
    
    def generate_recommendation_report(self, scores: List[PathScore], 
                                      input_data: PositioningCriteria) -> Dict:
        """生成完整的推荐报告"""
        top_choice = scores[0]
        second_choice = scores[1]
        
        report = {
            "executive_summary": {
                "recommended_path": top_choice.path.value,
                "confidence": top_choice.total_score,
                "recommendation": top_choice.recommendation_strength,
                "alternative": second_choice.path.value if second_choice.total_score > 0.5 else None
            },
            "detailed_scoring": [
                {
                    "path": s.path.value,
                    "total_score": s.total_score,
                    "dimension_breakdown": s.dimension_scores,
                    "strength": s.recommendation_strength
                } for s in scores
            ],
            "sensitivity_analysis": self._sensitivity_analysis(scores),
            "implementation_roadmap": self._generate_roadmap(top_choice.path),
            "risk_mitigation": self._risk_mitigation(top_choice.path, input_data)
        }
        
        return report
    
    def _sensitivity_analysis(self, scores: List[PathScore]) -> Dict:
        """敏感性分析：如果权重变化，推荐是否会改变"""
        original_winner = scores[0].path
        analysis = {
            "stable_recommendation": True,
            "critical_factors": []
        }
        
        # 检查第二名与第一名的差距
        if len(scores) > 1 and (scores[0].total_score - scores[1].total_score) < 0.1:
            analysis["stable_recommendation"] = False
            analysis["critical_factors"].append("市场与资源权重微小变化可能导致推荐改变")
        
        return analysis
    
    def _generate_roadmap(self, path: PositioningPath) -> List[str]:
        """生成实施路线图"""
        if path == PositioningPath.PATH_A:
            return [
                "M1-3: 核心算法研发与专利布局",
                "M4-6: MVP技术验证与封闭测试",
                "M7-9: 技术壁垒构建与标准制定",
                "M10-12: 商业化试点与规模化"
            ]
        elif path == PositioningPath.PATH_B:
            return [
                "M1-2: 服务流程设计与团队组建",
                "M3-4: 种子客户获取与反馈迭代",
                "M5-6: 运营体系标准化",
                "M7-9: 规模化获客与品牌建立"
            ]
        else:
            return [
                "M1-3: 平台架构设计与供应商招募",
                "M4-6: 冷启动策略与双边网络构建",
                "M7-9: 网络效应强化与数据积累",
                "M10-12: 盈利模式验证与扩张"
            ]
    
    def _risk_mitigation(self, path: PositioningPath, 
                       input_data: PositioningCriteria) -> List[str]:
        """风险缓解建议"""
        risks = []
        
        if input_data.regulatory_risk > 0.5:
            risks.append("优先完成合规审查，建立政府关系团队")
        
        if path == PositioningPath.PATH_A and input_data.resource_requirement > 0.7:
            risks.append("技术路径资源消耗大，建议分阶段融资或寻找战略投资者")
        
        if path == PositioningPath.PATH_C and input_data.competitive_intensity > 0.6:
            risks.append("平台路径面临激烈竞争，需明确差异化定位（垂直领域/特定人群）")
        
        return risks

# === 验收测试 ===
def test_positioning_assistant():
    """B6验收测试：输入3个维度，输出A/B/C路径评分"""
    
    assistant = ProductPositioningAssistant()
    
    # 场景：AI合伙人咨询平台（市场空间大，资源中等，时间窗口紧）
    input_data = PositioningCriteria(
        market_space=0.85,        # 市场空间大（AI咨询市场增长快）
        growth_rate=0.35,         # 年增长率35%
        resource_requirement=0.6, # 资源需求中等（不需要重资产）
        time_to_market=6,         # 希望6个月内上市
        competitive_intensity=0.7,  # 竞争激烈（已有头部玩家）
        regulatory_risk=0.3,      # 合规风险中等（AI监管趋严）
        team_capability_match=0.75  # 团队有AI+咨询背景
    )
    
    scores = assistant.evaluate_paths(input_data)
    report = assistant.generate_recommendation_report(scores, input_data)
    
    print("=" * 70)
    print("产品定位决策辅助报告")
    print("=" * 70)
    print(json.dumps(report, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))
    
    # 验证
    assert len(scores) == 3, "必须评估3条路径"
    assert all(0 <= s.total_score <= 1 for s in scores), "评分必须在0-1之间"
    assert scores[0].total_score >= scores[1].total_score >= scores[2].total_score, "必须按分数排序"
    assert "recommended_path" in report["executive_summary"], "必须有明确推荐"
    
    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print("路径排序：")
    for i, s in enumerate(scores, 1):
        print(f"{i}. {s.path.value}: {s.total_score:.3f} ({s.recommendation_strength})")
