P2-6: 最小可行满意解组织评估检查表 (B7) - 完整版

第一性原理洞察：
MVSR（Minimum Viable Satisficing Organization）的本质是反脆弱性组织设计。不是追求最优（Optimization），而是确保在不确定性下的最低限度可接受性（Satisficing）。10项检查点对应组织的认知、情感、结构、流程四维健康度。

# === mvsr_assessment.py (完整生产版) ===
import json
import sqlite3
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple, Set
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum
from datetime import datetime
import numpy as np

class OrganizationDimension(Enum):
    COGNITIVE = "cognitive"      # 认知层：决策质量、信息处理
    EMOTIONAL = "emotional"      # 情感层：心理安全、信任储备
    STRUCTURAL = "structural"    # 结构层：角色清晰、冗余设计
    PROCESS = "process"          # 流程层：闭环速度、迭代频率

class RiskLevel(Enum):
    CRITICAL = "critical"        # 致命风险（必须立即修复）
    HIGH = "high"                # 高风险（7天内修复）
    MEDIUM = "medium"            # 中等风险（30天内改进）
    LOW = "low"                  # 低风险（持续优化）

@dataclass
class CheckPoint:
    """评估检查点定义"""
    id: str
    name: str
    dimension: OrganizationDimension
    description: str
    weight: float  # 权重0-1
    validation_criteria: Dict[str, any]  # 验证标准
    risk_level: RiskLevel
    auto_checkable: bool  # 是否可自动检测

@dataclass
class AssessmentResult:
    """评估结果"""
    overall_score: float  # 0-1
    compliance_rating: str  # 优秀/良好/待改进/高风险
    dimension_scores: Dict[OrganizationDimension, float]
    checkpoint_details: List[Dict]
    failed_critical: List[str]
    improvement_roadmap: List[Dict]
    next_assessment_date: str

class MVSRAssessmentEngine:
    """
    最小可行满意解组织评估引擎（B7完整版）
    
    第一性原理设计：
    1. Satisficing而非Optimizing：寻找"足够好"而非"最好"
    2. 反脆弱性：压力源暴露弱点而非摧毁系统
    3. 实时性：评估与改进闭环<48小时
    
    10项检查点设计依据：
    - 基于Herb Simon的满意决策理论
    - 结合华人组织高语境特征
    - 适配AI-人机混合组织形态
    """
    
    # 10项核心检查点（MVSR标准）
    CHECKPOINTS = [
        CheckPoint(
            id="CP001",
            name="决策延迟",
            dimension=OrganizationDimension.COGNITIVE,
            description="重大决策（涉及>20%资源或战略方向）是否在48小时内形成闭环",
            weight=0.15,
            validation_criteria={
                "threshold_hours": 48,
                "sample_size": 5,  # 抽样检查最近5个决策
                "pass_rate": 0.8   # 80%通过即为合格
            },
            risk_level=RiskLevel.CRITICAL,
            auto_checkable=True
        ),
        CheckPoint(
            id="CP002",
            name="信息透明",
            dimension=OrganizationDimension.COGNITIVE,
            description="关键信息（财务/战略/风险）是否对所有决策相关合伙人可见",
            weight=0.15,
            validation_criteria={
                "info_categories": ["financial", "strategic", "risk", "operational"],
                "access_delay_hours": 24,  # 信息延迟<24小时
                "transparency_score_threshold": 0.8
            },
            risk_level=RiskLevel.CRITICAL,
            auto_checkable=True
        ),
        CheckPoint(
            id="CP003",
            name="冲突解决",
            dimension=OrganizationDimension.EMOTIONAL,
            description="是否存在明确的冲突升级机制（从1对1沟通到第三方调解）",
            weight=0.15,
            validation_criteria={
                "escalation_levels": 3,  # 至少3级升级机制
                "max_resolution_hours": 168,  # 7天内解决
                "documentation_required": True
            },
            risk_level=RiskLevel.HIGH,
            auto_checkable=False  # 需人工确认机制存在
        ),
        CheckPoint(
            id="CP004",
            name="退出路径",
            dimension=OrganizationDimension.STRUCTURAL,
            description="是否预先约定退出条款（Good/Bad Leaver标准、股权处理、竞业限制）",
            weight=0.15,
            validation_criteria={
                "leaver_clause_documented": True,
                "valuation_method_defined": True,
                "notice_period_months": 3,
                "buyback_funding_reserved": True
            },
            risk_level=RiskLevel.CRITICAL,
            auto_checkable=True
        ),
        CheckPoint(
            id="CP005",
            name="贡献评估",
            dimension=OrganizationDimension.COGNITIVE,
            description="是否有透明的贡献度量系统（非仅主观评价）",
            weight=0.10,
            validation_criteria={
                "metrics_objective_ratio": 0.6,  # 60%客观指标
                "review_frequency_months": 3,    # 季度回顾
                "appeal_mechanism_exists": True
            },
            risk_level=RiskLevel.MEDIUM,
            auto_checkable=True
        ),
        CheckPoint(
            id="CP006",
            name="风险分散",
            dimension=OrganizationDimension.STRUCTURAL,
            description="关键知识/关系/资源是否有双人备份（Bus Factor≥2）",
            weight=0.10,
            validation_criteria={
                "min_bus_factor": 2,  # 至少2人掌握
                "critical_areas": ["knowledge", "relationships", "resources"],
                "documentation_completeness": 0.8
            },
            risk_level=RiskLevel.HIGH,
            auto_checkable=True
        ),
        CheckPoint(
            id="CP007",
            name="心理安全",
            dimension=OrganizationDimension.EMOTIONAL,
            description="合伙人是否能安全表达异议（无报复恐惧）",
            weight=0.10,
            validation_criteria={
                "psychological_safety_score": 0.7,  # 基于匿名调查
                "dissent_recognition_instances": 3,  # 近3个月异议被采纳案例
                "retaliation_incidents": 0  # 报复事件必须为0
            },
            risk_level=RiskLevel.HIGH,
            auto_checkable=False  # 需人工调查
        ),
        CheckPoint(
            id="CP008",
            name="资源冗余",
            dimension=OrganizationDimension.PROCESS,
            description="是否有20%资源缓冲应对突发（时间/资金/人力）",
            weight=0.05,
            validation_criteria={
                "buffer_ratio": 0.20,
                "buffer_types": ["time", "financial", "human"],
                "activation_protocol_defined": True
            },
            risk_level=RiskLevel.MEDIUM,
            auto_checkable=True
        ),
        CheckPoint(
            id="CP009",
            name="迭代频率",
            dimension=OrganizationDimension.PROCESS,
            description="是否每月复盘组织运作（流程/结构/文化）",
            weight=0.025,
            validation_criteria={
                "review_frequency_months": 1,
                "action_item_closure_rate": 0.8,
                "participation_rate": 0.9
            },
            risk_level=RiskLevel.LOW,
            auto_checkable=True
        ),
        CheckPoint(
            id="CP010",
            name="信任仪式",
            dimension=OrganizationDimension.EMOTIONAL,
            description="是否有定期的非功利性互动（饭局/团建/非正式交流）",
            weight=0.025,
            validation_criteria={
                "informal_interaction_frequency_monthly": 2,  # 每月至少2次
                "non_transactional_ratio": 0.3,  # 30%时间非功利
                "high_context_signals_recognition": True  # 识别高语境信号
            },
            risk_level=RiskLevel.LOW,
            auto_checkable=False
        )
    ]
    
    def __init__(self, db_path: str = "mvsr_assessments.db"):
        self.db_path = db_path
        self._init_database()
    
    def _init_database(self):
        """初始化评估历史数据库"""
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        cursor.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS assessments (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                timestamp TEXT,
                team_id TEXT,
                overall_score REAL,
                dimension_scores TEXT,
                failed_checks TEXT,
                roadmap TEXT
            )
        """)
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()
    
    def assess(self, team_data: Dict, team_id: str = "default") -> AssessmentResult:
        """
        执行完整MVSR评估
        
        输入数据结构：
        {
            "decision_logs": [...],  # 决策记录（用于CP001）
            "info_access_logs": [...], # 信息访问日志（CP002）
            "conflict_records": [...], # 冲突记录（CP003）
            "exit_clause_exists": True,  # CP004
            "contribution_metrics": {...}, # CP005
            "knowledge_backup": {...}, # CP006
            "psychological_safety_survey": {...}, # CP007
            "resource_buffer": {...}, # CP008
            "review_records": [...], # CP009
            "informal_interactions": [...] # CP010
        }
        """
        checkpoint_results = []
        dimension_scores = {dim: [] for dim in OrganizationDimension}
        failed_critical = []
        
        # 逐检查点评估
        for cp in self.CHECKPOINTS:
            result = self._evaluate_checkpoint(cp, team_data)
            checkpoint_results.append(result)
            
            # 按维度聚合
            dimension_scores[cp.dimension].append(result["score"] * cp.weight)
            
            # 记录关键失败
            if not result["passed"] and cp.risk_level == RiskLevel.CRITICAL:
                failed_critical.append(cp.id)
        
        # 计算维度分
        dimension_final = {}
        for dim, scores in dimension_scores.items():
            total_weight = sum(cp.weight for cp in self.CHECKPOINTS if cp.dimension == dim)
            if total_weight > 0:
                dimension_final[dim] = sum(scores) / total_weight
            else:
                dimension_final[dim] = 0
        
        # 综合得分（加权平均）
        overall = sum(r["score"] * r["weight"] for r in checkpoint_results)
        
        # 评级
        rating = self._calculate_rating(overall, failed_critical)
        
        # 生成改进步骤
        roadmap = self._generate_roadmap(checkpoint_results, team_data)
        
        result = AssessmentResult(
            overall_score=round(overall, 3),
            compliance_rating=rating,
            dimension_scores={k: round(v, 3) for k, v in dimension_final.items()},
            checkpoint_details=checkpoint_results,
            failed_critical=failed_critical,
            improvement_roadmap=roadmap,
            next_assessment_date=(datetime.now().replace(day=1) + __import__('dateutil.relativedelta').relativedelta(months=1)).isoformat()
        )
        
        # 持久化
        self._save_assessment(team_id, result)
        
        return result
    
    def _evaluate_checkpoint(self, cp: CheckPoint, data: Dict) -> Dict:
        """评估单个检查点"""
        # 根据检查点ID分发到具体评估逻辑
        eval_methods = {
            "CP001": self._eval_decision_latency,
            "CP002": self._eval_info_transparency,
            "CP003": self._eval_conflict_resolution,
            "CP004": self._eval_exit_path,
            "CP005": self._eval_contribution_assessment,
            "CP006": self._eval_risk_distribution,
            "CP007": self._eval_psychological_safety,
            "CP008": self._eval_resource_buffer,
            "CP009": self._eval_iteration_frequency,
            "CP010": self._eval_trust_rituals
        }
        
        method = eval_methods.get(cp.id, lambda cp, data: {"score": 0.5, "evidence": "默认评估"})
        raw_result = method(cp, data)
        
        passed = raw_result["score"] >= 0.6  # 60分及格
        
        return {
            "id": cp.id,
            "name": cp.name,
            "dimension": cp.dimension.value,
            "score": round(raw_result["score"], 3),
            "weight": cp.weight,
            "passed": passed,
            "risk_level": cp.risk_level.value,
            "evidence": raw_result.get("evidence", ""),
            "recommendation": self._generate_checkpoint_recommendation(cp, raw_result["score"]) if not passed else ""
        }
    
    def _eval_decision_latency(self, cp: CheckPoint, data: Dict) -> Dict:
        """CP001: 决策延迟评估"""
        logs = data.get("decision_logs", [])
        if not logs:
            return {"score": 0, "evidence": "无决策记录"}
        
        threshold = cp.validation_criteria["threshold_hours"]
        recent_logs = logs[-cp.validation_criteria["sample_size"]:]
        
        timely_count = sum(1 for log in recent_logs 
                          if log.get("decision_hours", 999) <= threshold)
        
        pass_rate = timely_count / len(recent_logs)
        score = min(pass_rate / cp.validation_criteria["pass_rate"], 1.0)
        
        return {
            "score": score,
            "evidence": f"最近{len(recent_logs)}个决策，{timely_count}个在{threshold}小时内闭环，通过率{pass_rate:.0%}"
        }
    
    def _eval_info_transparency(self, cp: CheckPoint, data: Dict) -> Dict:
        """CP002: 信息透明度评估"""
        logs = data.get("info_access_logs", [])
        categories = cp.validation_criteria["info_categories"]
        
        if not logs:
            return {"score": 0.5, "evidence": "无访问日志，默认中等"}
        
        # 计算信息延迟
        delays = [log.get("access_delay_hours", 48) for log in logs 
                 if log.get("info_type") in categories]
        
        if not delays:
            return {"score": 0, "evidence": "关键类别信息无访问记录"}
        
        avg_delay = sum(delays) / len(delays)
        threshold = cp.validation_criteria["access_delay_hours"]
        
        # 延迟越短分越高
        score = max(0, 1 - (avg_delay / threshold))
        
        return {
            "score": score,
            "evidence": f"关键信息平均延迟{avg_delay:.1f}小时（阈值{threshold}小时）"
        }
    
    def _eval_conflict_resolution(self, cp: CheckPoint, data: Dict) -> Dict:
        """CP003: 冲突解决机制"""
        records = data.get("conflict_records", [])
        criteria = cp.validation_criteria
        
        # 检查升级机制存在性（人工确认）
        mechanism_exists = data.get("escalation_protocol_documented", False)
        if not mechanism_exists:
            return {"score": 0.3, "evidence": "未确认冲突升级机制文档"}
        
        # 检查解决时效
        if not records:
            return {"score": 0.8, "evidence": "无冲突记录（可能是好事或压抑）"}
        
        resolved_in_time = sum(1 for r in records 
                                if r.get("resolved", False) and 
                                r.get("resolution_hours", 9999) <= criteria["max_resolution_hours"])
        
        score = (resolved_in_time / len(records)) * 0.5 + 0.5  # 基础分0.5
        
        return {
            "score": score,
            "evidence": f"{len(records)}次冲突，{resolved_in_time}次在7天内解决"
        }
    
    def _eval_exit_path(self, cp: CheckPoint, data: Dict) -> Dict:
        """CP004: 退出路径"""
        if not data.get("exit_clause_exists", False):
            return {"score": 0, "evidence": "无退出条款文档", "critical_fail": True}
        
        score = 1.0
        checks = []
        
        if data.get("leaver_clause_documented"):
            checks.append("Leaver条款已文档化")
        else:
            score -= 0.3
            
        if data.get("valuation_method_defined"):
            checks.append("估值方法已定义")
        else:
            score -= 0.3
            
        if data.get("notice_period_months", 0) >= cp.validation_criteria["notice_period_months"]:
            checks.append(f"通知期≥{cp.validation_criteria['notice_period_months']}个月")
        else:
            score -= 0.2
        
        return {
            "score": max(score, 0),
            "evidence": "；".join(checks) if checks else "关键要素缺失"
        }
    
    def _eval_contribution_assessment(self, cp: CheckPoint, data: Dict) -> Dict:
        """CP005: 贡献评估透明度"""
        metrics = data.get("contribution_metrics", {})
        
        objective_ratio = metrics.get("objective_ratio", 0)
        target_ratio = cp.validation_criteria["metrics_objective_ratio"]
        
        score = min(objective_ratio / target_ratio, 1.0) if target_ratio > 0 else 0
        
        return {
            "score": score,
            "evidence": f"客观指标占比{objective_ratio:.0%}（目标{target_ratio:.0%}）"
        }
    
    def _eval_risk_distribution(self, cp: CheckPoint, data: Dict) -> Dict:
        """CP006: 风险分散（Bus Factor）"""
        backup = data.get("knowledge_backup", {})
        critical_areas = cp.validation_criteria["critical_areas"]
        
        scores = []
        for area in critical_areas:
            bus_factor = backup.get(area, {}).get("bus_factor", 1)
            target = cp.validation_criteria["min_bus_factor"]
            area_score = min(bus_factor / target, 1.0)
            scores.append(area_score)
        
        avg_score = sum(scores) / len(scores) if scores else 0
        
        return {
            "score": avg_score,
            "evidence": f"关键领域Bus Factor: {', '.join([f'{a}={backup.get(a,{}).get('bus_factor',1)}' for a in critical_areas])}"
        }
    
    def _eval_psychological_safety(self, cp: CheckPoint, data: Dict) -> Dict:
        """CP007: 心理安全"""
        survey = data.get("psychological_safety_survey", {})
        
        # 简化为基于匿名调查分数
        score = survey.get("average_score", 0.5)
        incidents = data.get("retaliation_incidents", 0)
        
        if incidents > 0:
            score = 0  # 有报复事件直接0分
        
        return {
            "score": score,
            "evidence": f"心理安全评分{score:.2f}，报复事件{incidents}次"
        }
    
    def _eval_resource_buffer(self, cp: CheckPoint, data: Dict) -> Dict:
        """CP008: 资源冗余"""
        buffer = data.get("resource_buffer", {})
        target_ratio = cp.validation_criteria["buffer_ratio"]
        
        actual_ratio = buffer.get("current_ratio", 0)
        score = min(actual_ratio / target_ratio, 1.0)
        
        return {
            "score": score,
            "evidence": f"当前冗余率{actual_ratio:.0%}（目标{target_ratio:.0%}）"
        }
    
    def _eval_iteration_frequency(self, cp: CheckPoint, data: Dict) -> Dict:
        """CP009: 迭代频率"""
        records = data.get("review_records", [])
        
        if not records:
            return {"score": 0, "evidence": "无复盘记录"}
        
        # 检查最近3个月是否每月都有复盘
        recent_months = len([r for r in records if r.get("monthly_review", False)])
        score = min(recent_months / 3, 1.0) if len(records) >= 3 else len(records) / 3
        
        return {
            "score": score,
            "evidence": f"近3个月复盘次数: {recent_months}"
        }
    
    def _eval_trust_rituals(self, cp: CheckPoint, data: Dict) -> Dict:
        """CP010: 信任仪式"""
        interactions = data.get("informal_interactions", [])
        
        # 统计近3个月非正式互动
        recent_count = len([i for i in interactions if i.get("in_last_3_months", False)])
        target_monthly = cp.validation_criteria["informal_interaction_frequency_monthly"]
        target_total = target_monthly * 3
        
        score = min(recent_count / target_total, 1.0)
        
        return {
            "score": score,
            "evidence": f"近3个月非正式互动{recent_count}次（目标{target_total}次）"
        }
    
    def _generate_checkpoint_recommendation(self, cp: CheckPoint, score: float) -> str:
        """生成针对单个检查点的改进建议"""
        recommendations = {
            "CP001": "建立决策时限制度，重大决策设置48小时倒计时，超时自动升级",
            "CP002": "部署信息透明仪表板，关键指标24小时内对所有合伙人可见",
            "CP003": "制定冲突解决SOP：1对1沟通→小组调解→外部仲裁三级机制",
            "CP004": "立即起草退出协议，明确Good/Bad Leaver标准和估值方法",
            "CP005": "引入OKR或KPI系统，确保60%以上指标客观可量化",
            "CP006": "实施知识双备份制度，关键岗位培养副手，文档化核心流程",
            "CP007": "开展心理安全培训，建立匿名反馈渠道，零容忍报复行为",
            "CP008": "预留20%资源缓冲，建立应急基金和机动时间池",
            "CP009": "固定每月最后一个周五为组织复盘日，形成仪式",
            "CP010": "每月至少组织2次非功利性聚会（饭局/运动/文化活动）"
        }
        
        base_rec = recommendations.get(cp.id, "需针对性改进")
        urgency = "【紧急】" if cp.risk_level == RiskLevel.CRITICAL else "【重要】" if cp.risk_level == RiskLevel.HIGH else ""
        
        return f"{urgency}{base_rec}（当前得分{score:.0%}）"
    
    def _calculate_rating(self, overall: float, failed_critical: List[str]) -> str:
        """计算合规评级"""
        if failed_critical:
            return "高风险（关键项失败）"
        if overall >= 0.9:
            return "优秀（MVSR充分满足）"
        elif overall >= 0.75:
            return "良好（基本满足，可优化）"
        elif overall >= 0.6:
            return "待改进（存在明显缺陷）"
        else:
            return "高风险（需立即整改）"
    
    def _generate_roadmap(self, results: List[Dict], data: Dict) -> List[Dict]:
        """生成改进步骤（按优先级排序）"""
        failed = [r for r in results if not r["passed"]]
        
        # 按风险等级和权重排序
        priority_order = {
            RiskLevel.CRITICAL.value: 0,
            RiskLevel.HIGH.value: 1,
            RiskLevel.MEDIUM.value: 2,
            RiskLevel.LOW.value: 3
        }
        
        failed.sort(key=lambda x: (priority_order.get(x["risk_level"], 99), -x["weight"]))
        
        roadmap = []
        for i, item in enumerate(failed[:5], 1):  # 前5优先
            roadmap.append({
                "step": i,
                "checkpoint": item["name"],
                "priority": item["risk_level"],
                "target_score": 0.8,
                "current_score": item["score"],
                "action": item["recommendation"],
                "timeline": "立即" if item["risk_level"] == RiskLevel.CRITICAL.value else "7天内" if item["risk_level"] == RiskLevel.HIGH.value else "30天内"
            })
        
        return roadmap
    
    def _save_assessment(self, team_id: str, result: AssessmentResult):
        """保存评估历史"""
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        cursor.execute("""
            INSERT INTO assessments 
            (timestamp, team_id, overall_score, dimension_scores, failed_checks, roadmap)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        """, (
            datetime.now().isoformat(),
            team_id,
            result.overall_score,
            json.dumps(result.dimension_scores),
            json.dumps(result.failed_critical),
            json.dumps(result.improvement_roadmap)
        ))
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()

# === 验收测试 ===
def test_mvsr_assessment():
    """B7验收测试：10项检查点，输出合规度评分"""
    
    engine = MVSRAssessmentEngine()
    
    # 模拟团队数据
    team_data = {
        "decision_logs": [
            {"decision_hours": 24}, {"decision_hours": 72}, {"decision_hours": 36},
            {"decision_hours": 48}, {"decision_hours": 12}
        ],
        "info_access_logs": [
            {"info_type": "financial", "access_delay_hours": 12},
            {"info_type": "strategic", "access_delay_hours": 48}
        ],
        "conflict_records": [
            {"resolved": True, "resolution_hours": 120}
        ],
        "exit_clause_exists": True,
        "leaver_clause_documented": True,
        "valuation_method_defined": True,
        "notice_period_months": 3,
        "contribution_metrics": {"objective_ratio": 0.7},
        "knowledge_backup": {
            "knowledge": {"bus_factor": 2},
            "relationships": {"bus_factor": 1},  # 单点故障
            "resources": {"bus_factor": 2}
        },
        "psychological_safety_survey": {"average_score": 0.75},
        "retaliation_incidents": 0,
        "resource_buffer": {"current_ratio": 0.15},  # 不足20%
        "review_records": [{"monthly_review": True}, {"monthly_review": True}],
        "informal_interactions": [{"in_last_3_months": True}] * 4,
        "escalation_protocol_documented": True
    }
    
    result = engine.assess(team_data, "team_alpha")
    
    print("=" * 70)
    print("MVSR组织评估报告")
    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"综合评分: {result.overall_score} ({result.compliance_rating})")
    print(f"维度得分:")
    for dim, score in result.dimension_scores.items():
        print(f"  {dim.value}: {score}")
    print(f"关键失败项: {result.failed_critical}")
    print(f"改进步骤:")
    for step in result.improvement_roadmap:
        print(f"  {step['step']}. [{step['timeline']}] {step['checkpoint']}: {step['action'][:50]}...")
    
    # 断言
    assert 0 <= result.overall_score <= 1
    assert len(result.checkpoint_details) == 10, "必须检查10项"
    assert len(result.dimension_scores) == 4, "必须4个维度"
    assert isinstance(result.improvement_roadmap, list)
    
    print("\n✅ B7验收通过：MVSR组织评估检查表工作正常")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    test_mvsr_assessment()

P2-7: 法律文档生成器 (C5) - 完整版

第一性原理洞察：
法律文档的本质是条件-义务的确定性结构化。生成式AI的法律风险在于”幻觉条款”，本系统采用参数化模板+严格校验，确保100%基于预设法律实体。

# === legal_doc_generator.py (完整生产版) ===
import re
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from enum import Enum

class DocType(Enum):
    PARTNERSHIP_AGREEMENT = "partnership_agreement"  # 合伙协议
    NDA = "nda"                                         # 保密协议
    IP_ASSIGNMENT = "ip_assignment"                     # 知识产权归属

class PartnerType(Enum):
    INDIVIDUAL = "individual"
    CORPORATION = "corporation"
    LP = "limited_partnership"

@dataclass
class ValidationRule:
    field: str
    required: bool
    pattern: Optional[str] = None
    min_value: Optional[float] = None
    max_value: Optional[float] = None
    enum_values: Optional[List[str]] = None

class LegalDocGenerator:
    """
    法律文档生成器（C5完整版）
    
    安全设计：
    1. 零自由生成：所有文本来自预设模板
    2. 参数白名单：仅允许预定义字段
    3. 自动校验：类型/范围/格式检查
    4. 风险提示：自动生成法律免责声明
    """
    
    # 模板库（含完整法律条款）
    TEMPLATES = {
        DocType.PARTNERSHIP_AGREEMENT: {
            "title": "有限合伙协议",
            "sections": [
                {
                    "id": "Preamble",
                    "title": "鉴于条款",
                    "template": """鉴于：
1. 甲方{partner_a_name}（{partner_a_type}），身份证号/统一社会信用代码{partner_a_id}；
2. 乙方{partner_b_name}（{partner_b_type}），身份证号/统一社会信用代码{partner_b_id}；
双方本着平等互利、风险共担的原则，依据《中华人民共和国合伙企业法》及相关法律法规，经友好协商，达成如下协议。"""
                },
                {
                    "id": "Article1",
                    "title": "第一条 合伙目的与经营范围",
                    "template": """合伙目的：{business_purpose}
经营范围：{business_scope}（以工商登记为准）
经营期限：{term_years}年，自营业执照签发之日起计算。
合伙企业的注册地址为：{registered_address}。"""
                },
                {
                    "id": "Article2",
                    "title": "第二条 出资方式、数额与缴付期限",
                    "template": """2.1 合伙人出资方式与数额：
    甲方：货币出资人民币{a_contribution_cny}万元，占出资比例{a_ratio}%，于{contribution_deadline}前缴足；
    乙方：货币出资人民币{b_contribution_cny}万元，占出资比例{b_ratio}%，于{contribution_deadline}前缴足。

2.2 非货币出资评估：
如以知识产权、实物等非货币财产出资，须经全体合伙人协商一致评估作价，并依法办理财产权转移手续。

2.3 出资违约责任：
逾期缴付出资的合伙人应按日万分之五支付违约金，逾期超过{default_days}日的，视为自动退伙。"""
                },
                {
                    "id": "Article3",
                    "title": "第三条 利润分配与亏损分担",
                    "template": """3.1 利润分配：
合伙企业的利润分配按照以下顺序进行：
(1) 先提取{reserve_ratio}%作为企业发展基金；
(2) 剩余部分按出资比例分配，即甲方{a_ratio}%，乙方{b_ratio}%；
(3) 分配时间为每个财务年度结束后{distribution_days}日内。

3.2 亏损分担：
亏损按出资比例分担，但合伙人以个人财产承担无限连带责任（适用于普通合伙人）或以其出资额为限承担责任（适用于有限合伙人）。

3.3 禁止行为：
任何合伙人不得在合伙企业亏损时擅自分配利润，违者须退还所得并赔偿损失。"""
                },
                {
                    "id": "Article4",
                    "title": "第四条 事务执行与决策机制",
                    "template": """4.1 执行事务合伙人：
{executing_partner}为执行事务合伙人，对外代表合伙企业，其权限包括：
(1) 单笔不超过{single_decision_limit}万元的经营支出；
(2) 日常人事任免；
(3) 签署标的额不超过{contract_limit}万元的合同。

4.2 重大事项决策：
以下事项需经全体合伙人{decision_threshold}（如：一致/三分之二以上）同意：
(1) 处分合伙企业不动产；
(2) 变更合伙企业经营范围；
(3) 转让或处分合伙企业的知识产权；
(4) 以合伙企业名义为他人提供担保；
(5) 聘任合伙人以外的人担任经营管理人员。

4.3 否决权设置：
{decision_veto_description}"""
                },
                {
                    "id": "Article5",
                    "title": "第五条 入伙、退伙与出资转让",
                    "template": """5.1 入伙：
新合伙人入伙须经全体合伙人一致同意，并签订书面入伙协议。新合伙人对入伙前合伙企业债务承担连带责任。

5.2 退伙机制（Good Leaver/Bad Leaver条款）：
(a) Good Leaver情形：因退休、丧失行为能力、经全体合伙人同意的战略分歧而退伙的，视为善意退出；
    - 已成熟股权/出资份额按{good_leaver_valuation}估值由合伙企业或其他合伙人回购；
    - 回购款分{good_leaver_payment_terms}期支付；
    - 承担{good_leaver_non_compete}个月竞业限制义务，合伙企业按{good_leaver_compensation}%月薪支付补偿。

(b) Bad Leaver情形：因严重违反合伙协议、故意损害合伙企业利益、被追究刑事责任而退伙的，视为恶意退出；
    - 已成熟出资份额按原始出资额（人民币{bad_leaver_buyback_price}万元）回购；
    - 未成熟部分无偿收回；
    - 承担{bad_leaver_non_compete}个月竞业限制义务（无补偿）；
    - 赔偿因其行为造成的全部损失（包括直接损失和可得利益）。

5.3 出资份额转让：
合伙人向合伙人以外的人转让出资份额，须经其他合伙人过半数同意；其他合伙人享有优先购买权。"""
                },
                {
                    "id": "Article6",
                    "title": "第六条 财务、会计与信息披露",
                    "template": """6.1 财务制度：
合伙企业依照《会计法》和《企业会计准则》建立财务制度，会计年度为公历年度。

6.2 信息披露：
执行事务合伙人应于每月{reporting_day}日前向全体合伙人提供：
(1) 上月资产负债表；
(2) 损益表；
(3) 现金流量表；
(4) 重大事项说明。

6.3 审计权利：
任一合伙人有权聘请会计师事务所对合伙企业进行审计，费用由提出审计方先行垫付，如审计发现重大问题，费用由合伙企业承担。"""
                },
                {
                    "id": "Article7",
                    "title": "第七条 解散与清算",
                    "template": """7.1 解散事由：
(1) 合伙期限届满，合伙人决定不再经营；
(2) 全体合伙人决定解散；
(3) 依法被吊销营业执照、责令关闭或被撤销；
(4) 法律、行政法规规定的其他原因。

7.2 清算：
合伙企业解散，应当由清算人进行清算。清算人由全体合伙人担任；未能由全体合伙人担任清算人的，经全体合伙人过半数同意，可以自合伙企业解散事由出现后十五日内指定一个或者数个合伙人，或者委托第三人担任清算人。

7.3 清算财产分配：
清算财产在支付清算费用和职工工资、社会保险费用、法定补偿金以及缴纳所欠税款、清偿债务后的剩余财产，依照本协议第三条约定分配。"""
                },
                {
                    "id": "Article8",
                    "title": "第八条 争议解决与法律适用",
                    "template": """8.1 争议解决：
因本协议引起的或与本协议有关的任何争议，双方应友好协商解决；协商不成的，提交{arbitration_body}按照其届时有效的仲裁规则进行仲裁。仲裁裁决是终局的，对双方均有约束力。

8.2 法律适用：
本协议的订立、效力、解释、履行和争议解决均适用中华人民共和国法律（不包括港澳台地区法律）。

8.3 文本效力：
本协议一式{document_copies}份，合伙人各执一份，合伙企业留存一份，各份具有同等法律效力。"""
                },
                {
                    "id": "Signature",
                    "title": "签署页",
                    "template": """本协议由以下各方于{signing_date}签署：

甲方（签字/盖章）：_________________

乙方（签字/盖章）：_________________

(1) 披露时已为公众所知或之后非因接收方过错成为公众所知的信息；

(1) 向任何第三方披露；
(2) 用于本协议约定目的之外的任何用途；

2.2 例外披露：

本协议的任何修改须经双方书面签署。

6.3 转让：

4.2 后续收益：
如作品产生许可费、转让费、维权赔偿金，乙方应在收到后{revenue_share_days}日内向甲方支付{revenue_share_percent}%作为奖励（如有约定）。

4.3 税费：
甲方应自行承担因本协议产生的个人所得税等税费。"""                },
                {
                    "id": "Article5",
                    "title": "第五条 保密与竞业",
                    "template": """5.1 保密：

                {
                    "id": "Article6",
                    "title": "第六条 其他",
                    "template": """6.1 争议解决：提交{arbitration_body}仲裁。
6.2 法律适用：中华人民共和国法律。
6.3 文本：本协议一式两份，双方各执一份。

签署：
甲方（创作方）：{creator_name}          乙方（权利方）：{business_entity_name}
签字：_________________                 签字：_________________
日期：{signing_date}                    日期：{signing_date}"""
                }
            ]
        }
    }
    
    # 参数验证规则
    VALIDATION_RULES = {
        DocType.PARTNERSHIP_AGREEMENT: [
            ValidationRule("partner_a_name", True),
            ValidationRule("partner_a_id", True, pattern=r"^\d{17}[\dXx]$|^[A-Z0-9]{18}$"),  # 身份证或统一信用代码
            ValidationRule("partner_b_name", True),
            ValidationRule("partner_b_id", True, pattern=r"^\d{17}[\dXx]$|^[A-Z0-9]{18}$"),
            ValidationRule("a_contribution_cny", True, min_value=0.01),
            ValidationRule("a_ratio", True, min_value=0, max_value=100),
            ValidationRule("b_contribution_cny", True, min_value=0.01),
            ValidationRule("b_ratio", True, min_value=0, max_value=100),
            ValidationRule("term_years", True, min_value=1, max_value=100),
        ],
        DocType.NDA: [
            ValidationRule("discloser_name", True),
            ValidationRule("recipient_name", True),
            ValidationRule("confidentiality_years", True, min_value=1, max_value=10),
            ValidationRule("liquidated_damages_cny", True, min_value=1000),
        ],
        DocType.IP_ASSIGNMENT: [
            ValidationRule("creator_name", True),
            ValidationRule("business_entity_name", True),
            ValidationRule("consideration_cny", True, min_value=0),
        ]
    }
    
    def generate(self, doc_type: DocType, params: Dict) -> Dict:
        """
        生成法律文档
        
        流程：
        1. 参数白名单校验
        2. 业务规则校验（如出资比例之和=100）
        3. 模板填充
        4. 生成风险提示
        """
        # 1. 验证参数
        errors = self._validate_params(doc_type, params)
        if errors:
            return {
                "success": False,
                "errors": errors,
                "document": None,
                "warning": "参数验证失败，未生成文档"
            }
        
        # 2. 获取模板
        template_config = self.TEMPLATES[doc_type]
        
        # 3. 填充各章节
        sections = []
        full_text_parts = []
        
        for section in template_config["sections"]:
            try:
                filled = section["template"].format(**params)
                sections.append({
                    "id": section["id"],
                    "title": section["title"],
                    "content": filled
                })
                full_text_parts.append(f"\n\n{section['title']}\n{filled}")
            except KeyError as e:
                return {
                    "success": False,
                    "errors": [f"模板参数缺失: {str(e)}"],
                    "document": None
                }
        
        full_document = f"{template_config['title']}\n{'='*50}{''.join(full_text_parts)}"
        
        # 4. 生成元数据
        metadata = {
            "doc_type": doc_type.value,
            "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "version": "1.0",
            "param_summary": {k: str(v)[:50] for k, v in params.items()},
            "section_count": len(sections),
            "word_count": len(full_document)
        }
        
        return {
            "success": True,
            "errors": [],
            "document": {
                "title": template_config["title"],
                "full_text": full_document,
                "sections": sections,
                "metadata": metadata
            },
            "warning": self._generate_legal_disclaimer(doc_type),
            "next_steps": [
                "经专业律师审核",
                "根据具体情况调整条款",
                "双方充分理解后签署",
                "妥善保管原件"
            ]
        }
    
    def _validate_params(self, doc_type: DocType, params: Dict) -> List[str]:
        """参数验证"""
        errors = []
        rules = self.VALIDATION_RULES.get(doc_type, [])
        
        for rule in rules:
            value = params.get(rule.field)
            
            if rule.required and (value is None or value == ""):
                errors.append(f"必填字段缺失: {rule.field}")
                continue
            
            if value is not None:
                # 正则验证
                if rule.pattern and isinstance(value, str):
                    if not re.match(rule.pattern, value):
                        errors.append(f"字段{rule.field}格式错误")
                
                # 数值范围
                if isinstance(value, (int, float)):
                    if rule.min_value is not None and value < rule.min_value:
                        errors.append(f"字段{rule.field}低于最小值{rule.min_value}")
                    if rule.max_value is not None and value > rule.max_value:
                        errors.append(f"字段{rule.field}超过最大值{rule.max_value}")
        
        # 业务规则：出资比例之和检查
        if doc_type == DocType.PARTNERSHIP_AGREEMENT:
            a_ratio = params.get("a_ratio", 0)
            b_ratio = params.get("b_ratio", 0)
            if abs(a_ratio + b_ratio - 100) > 0.01:
                errors.append(f"出资比例之和必须等于100%，当前为{a_ratio + b_ratio}%")
        
        return errors
    
    def _generate_legal_disclaimer(self, doc_type: DocType) -> str:
        """生成法律免责声明"""
        disclaimers = {
            DocType.PARTNERSHIP_AGREEMENT: """
【重要法律声明】
本协议模板基于《中华人民共和国合伙企业法》一般性规定生成，仅供参考。
⚠️ 使用提示：
1. 合伙协议涉及重大财产权益，强烈建议经专业律师根据具体情况审核修改；
2. 不同地区（如深圳前海、海南自贸港）可能有特殊规定，需调整适用；
3. 涉及外商投资、国有资产的，需符合特别法规定；
4. 本生成器不提供法律意见，使用者自行承担使用风险。
            """,
            DocType.NDA: """
【重要法律声明】
本保密协议模板适用于一般商业场景，对于特定行业（如军工、医药、上市公司）可能有更高保密要求。
⚠️ 使用提示：
1. 违约金数额需符合《民法典》第585条合理性要求，过高可能被法院调减；
2. 竞业限制条款需符合《劳动合同法》第24条（如适用）；
3. 跨境数据传输需遵守《数据出境安全评估办法》；
4. 建议根据保密信息的具体类型调整保密期限和范围。
            """,
            DocType.IP_ASSIGNMENT: """
【重要法律声明】
知识产权归属协议涉及复杂法律问题，包括职务发明认定、合作作品权利分割等。
⚠️ 使用提示：
1. 职务发明归属需符合《专利法》第6条及《专利法实施细则》第12条；
2. 软件著作权归属需考虑开源许可证传染性；
3. 高校、科研院所人员需注意兼职/成果转化的特殊规定；
4. 建议办理著作权登记、专利著录项目变更以对抗第三人。
            """
        }
        return disclaimers.get(doc_type, "请经专业律师审核后使用")

# === 验收测试 ===
def test_legal_doc_generator():
    """C5验收测试：3份标准协议模板可输出"""
    
    generator = LegalDocGenerator()
    
    # 测试1：合伙协议
    partnership_params = {
        "partner_a_name": "张三",
        "partner_a_id": "110101199001011234",
        "partner_a_type": "自然人",
        "partner_b_name": "李四",
        "partner_b_id": "110101199002022345",
        "partner_b_type": "自然人",
        "business_purpose": "共同经营人工智能咨询服务业务",
        "business_scope": "技术开发、技术咨询、技术服务",
        "term_years": 10,
        "registered_address": "北京市海淀区中关村大街1号",
        "a_contribution_cny": 50,
        "a_ratio": 60,
        "b_contribution_cny": 50,
        "b_ratio": 40,
        "contribution_deadline": "2026年12月31日",
        "default_days": 30,
        "reserve_ratio": 10,
        "distribution_days": 30,
        "executing_partner": "张三",
        "single_decision_limit": 10,
        "contract_limit": 50,
        "decision_threshold": "一致同意",
        "decision_veto_description": "甲方对技术路线决策拥有否决权",
        "good_leaver_valuation": "净资产值或最近一轮估值孰高",
        "good_leaver_payment_terms": 3,
        "good_leaver_non_compete": 6,
        "good_leaver_compensation": 30,
        "bad_leaver_buyback_price": 1.00,
        "bad_leaver_non_compete": 12,
        "reporting_day": 10,
        "arbitration_body": "北京仲裁委员会",
        "document_copies": 3,
        "signing_date": "2026年4月6日"
    }
    
    result1 = generator.generate(DocType.PARTNERSHIP_AGREEMENT, partnership_params)
    assert result1["success"], f"合伙协议生成失败: {result1['errors']}"
    assert len(result1["document"]["full_text"]) > 1000, "文档内容过短"
    assert "Good Leaver" in result1["document"]["full_text"], "应包含Leaver条款"
    print("✅ 合伙协议生成成功（含完整10个章节）")
    
    # 测试2：NDA
    nda_params = {
        "discloser_name": "甲方科技有限公司",
        "recipient_name": "乙方咨询工作室",
        "cooperation_purpose": "潜在商业合作评估",
        "confidentiality_years": 5,
        "liquidated_damages_cny": 50,
        "arbitration_body": "中国国际经济贸易仲裁委员会",
        "signing_date": "2026年4月6日"
    }
    
    result2 = generator.generate(DocType.NDA, nda_params)
    assert result2["success"], f"NDA生成失败: {result2['errors']}"
    assert "违约金" in result2["document"]["full_text"], "应包含违约责任"
    print("✅ 保密协议生成成功")
    
    # 测试3：IP归属协议
    ip_params = {
        "creator_name": "王五",
        "business_entity_name": "创新科技有限公司",
        "creation_description": "智能算法模块及相关技术文档",
        "consideration_cny": 10,
        "revenue_share_days": 30,
        "revenue_share_percent": 20,
        "non_compete_months": 12,
        "non_compete_compensation": 50,
        "cost_bearing_party": "乙方",
        "arbitration_body": "深圳国际仲裁院",
        "signing_date": "2026年4月6日"
    }
    
    result3 = generator.generate(DocType.IP_ASSIGNMENT, ip_params)
    assert result3["success"], f"IP协议生成失败: {result3['errors']}"
    assert "知识产权" in result3["document"]["full_text"], "应包含知识产权条款"
    print("✅ 知识产权归属协议生成成功")
    
    # 验证错误处理（出资比例之和不等于100）
    bad_params = partnership_params.copy()
    bad_params["b_ratio"] = 50  # 60+50=110
    result_error = generator.generate(DocType.PARTNERSHIP_AGREEMENT, bad_params)
    assert not result_error["success"], "应检测出比例错误"
    assert "必须等于100%" in str(result_error["errors"]), "应提示比例错误"
    print("✅ 参数验证工作正常")
    
    print("\n✅ C5验收通过：法律文档生成器（3模板完整版）工作正常")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    test_legal_doc_generator()

P2-8: 系统健康度指标看板 (C6) - 完整版

第一性原理洞察：
系统健康的本质是熵增的实时监控。通过热力学第二定律视角，将系统无序度（错误率、延迟方差、资源碎片度）量化为认知熵，并建立耗散结构（负熵流输入机制）。

# === system_health_dashboard.py (完整生产版) ===
import sqlite3
import json
import math
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple, Optional
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
import numpy as np
from collections import defaultdict

@dataclass
class MetricSeries:
    """时间序列指标"""
    timestamps: List[float]
    values: List[float]
    metric_name: str
    unit: str

@dataclass
class HealthReport:
    """健康度报告"""
    timestamp: str
    overall_health: float  # 0-100
    entropy_index: float   # 认知熵（越低越好）
    trend: str  # improving/stable/degrading
    dimension_health: Dict[str, float]
    alerts: List[Dict]
    recommendations: List[str]
    visualization_data: Dict

class SystemHealthDashboard:
    """
    系统健康度指标看板（C6完整版）
    
    第一性原理设计：
    1. 认知热力学：将系统视为认知耗散结构，监测熵增/负熵流
    2. 三层次监控：
       - L1：物理层（API延迟、错误率、资源使用）
       - L2：认知层（缓存命中率、降级频率、语义一致性）
       - L3：组织层（决策延迟、合伙人满意度、知识流失率）
    3. 熵增计算：基于香农熵 H = -Σp(x)logp(x)
    """
    
    def __init__(self, db_path: str = "system_metrics.db"):
        self.db_path = db_path
        self._init_database()
    
    def _init_database(self):
        """初始化指标数据库"""
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        
        # 指标原始数据表
        cursor.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS raw_metrics (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                timestamp REAL,
                metric_type TEXT,
                value REAL,
                component TEXT,
                metadata TEXT
            )
        """)
        
        # 熵值历史表
        cursor.execute("""
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS entropy_history (
                timestamp REAL,
                entropy_value REAL,
                component_breakdown TEXT
            )
        """)
        
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()
    
    def collect_metrics(self, 
                       api_latency_ms: List[float],
                       error_rates: List[float],
                       cache_hit_rates: List[float],
                       token_usage: List[int],
                       decision_delays: List[float],
                       cognitive_fallbacks: int) -> Dict:
        """
        收集多维度指标并计算健康度
        """
        timestamp = datetime.now().timestamp()
        
        # 存储原始指标
        self._store_raw_metrics(timestamp, {
            "api_latency": api_latency_ms,
            "error_rate": error_rates,
            "cache_hit": cache_hit_rates,
            "token_usage": token_usage,
            "decision_delay": decision_delays,
            "cognitive_fallback": [cognitive_fallbacks]
        })
        
        # 计算各维度健康度
        physical_health = self._calc_physical_health(api_latency_ms, error_rates)
        cognitive_health = self._calc_cognitive_health(cache_hit_rates, cognitive_fallbacks)
        organizational_health = self._calc_org_health(decision_delays, token_usage)
        
        # 计算认知熵（系统无序度）
        entropy = self._calculate_entropy(error_rates, api_latency_ms)
        
        # 趋势分析（与历史比较）
        trend = self._analyze_trend(entropy)
        
        # 生成告警
        alerts = self._generate_alerts(
            physical_health, cognitive_health, organizational_health, entropy
        )
        
        # 生成建议
        recommendations = self._generate_recommendations(alerts)
        
        # 可视化数据
        viz_data = self._prepare_visualization_data()
        
        report = HealthReport(
            timestamp=datetime.now().isoformat(),
            overall_health=round((physical_health + cognitive_health + organizational_health) / 3, 1),
            entropy_index=round(entropy, 3),
            trend=trend,
            dimension_health={
                "physical_layer": round(physical_health, 1),
                "cognitive_layer": round(cognitive_health, 1),
                "organizational_layer": round(organizational_health, 1)
            },
            alerts=alerts,
            recommendations=recommendations,
            visualization_data=viz_data
        )
        
        # 存储熵值历史
        self._store_entropy(timestamp, entropy, report.dimension_health)
        
        return report
    
    def _store_raw_metrics(self, timestamp: float, metrics: Dict[str, List[float]]):
        """存储原始指标到数据库"""
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        
        for metric_type, values in metrics.items():
            for value in values:
                cursor.execute("""
                    INSERT INTO raw_metrics (timestamp, metric_type, value, component, metadata)
                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                """, (timestamp, metric_type, value, "system", "{}"))
        
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()
    
    def _calc_physical_health(self, latencies: List[float], errors: List[float]) -> float:
        """物理层健康度（API基础设施）"""
        if not latencies or not errors:
            return 50.0
        
        # 延迟评分（P95<500ms为100分，>2000ms为0分）
        p95_latency = np.percentile(latencies, 95)
        latency_score = max(0, 100 - (p95_latency - 500) / 15) if p95_latency > 500 else 100
        
        # 错误率评分（<0.1%为100分，>5%为0分）
        avg_error = np.mean(errors)
        error_score = max(0, 100 - (avg_error - 0.1) * 20) if avg_error > 0.1 else 100
        
        return (latency_score * 0.6 + error_score * 0.4)
    
    def _calc_cognitive_health(self, cache_hits: List[float], fallbacks: int) -> float:
        """认知层健康度（AI系统质量）"""
        if not cache_hits:
            return 50.0
        
        # 缓存命中率评分（>80%为100分）
        avg_hit = np.mean(cache_hits)
        cache_score = min(avg_hit * 125, 100)
        
        # 降级频率评分（fallbacks越少越好）
        # 假设单日fallbacks>10次为不健康
        fallback_score = max(0, 100 - fallbacks * 10)
        
        return (cache_score * 0.7 + fallback_score * 0.3)
    
    def _calc_org_health(self, decision_delays: List[float], token_usage: List[int]) -> float:
        """组织层健康度（业务效能）"""
        if not decision_delays:
            return 50.0
        
        # 决策延迟评分（<48小时为100分）
        avg_delay = np.mean(decision_delays)
        delay_score = 100 if avg_delay <= 48 else max(0, 100 - (avg_delay - 48) / 2)
        
        # Token使用效率（单位决策消耗token数，越低越高效）
        # 假设平均每个决策消耗1000token为基准
        avg_tokens = np.mean(token_usage) if token_usage else 1000
        efficiency_score = max(0, 100 - (avg_tokens - 1000) / 50)
        
        return (delay_score * 0.6 + efficiency_score * 0.4)
    
    def _calculate_entropy(self, error_rates: List[float], latencies: List[float]) -> float:
        """
        计算系统认知熵（基于香农熵）
        
        将错误率和延迟视为概率分布，计算其无序度
        """
        if not error_rates or len(error_rates) < 2:
            return 0.5
        
        # 错误率熵
        # 将连续值离散化为 bins
        bins = 5
        hist, _ = np.histogram(error_rates, bins=bins, range=(0, max(error_rates + [0.01])))
        probs = hist / len(error_rates)
        probs = probs[probs > 0]  # 避免log(0)
        
        error_entropy = -np.sum(probs * np.log2(probs)) if len(probs) > 0 else 0
        
        # 延迟方差熵（高方差=高熵=不稳定）
        latency_cv = np.std(latencies) / (np.mean(latencies) + 1e-8)  # 变异系数
        latency_entropy = min(latency_cv, 1.0)  # 归一化到0-1
        
        # 综合认知熵（0-1，越高越无序）
        combined_entropy = (error_entropy / np.log2(bins)) * 0.6 + latency_entropy * 0.4
        
        return float(combined_entropy)
    
    def _analyze_trend(self, current_entropy: float) -> str:
        """分析熵增趋势"""
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        
        # 获取最近7天的平均熵值
        week_ago = (datetime.now() - timedelta(days=7)).timestamp()
        cursor.execute("""
            SELECT AVG(entropy_value) FROM entropy_history 
            WHERE timestamp > ?
        """, (week_ago,))
        
        result = cursor.fetchone()
        conn.close()
        
        if result and result[0]:
            avg_historical = result[0]
            diff = current_entropy - avg_historical
            
            if diff < -0.05:
                return "improving"  # 熵减（更有序）
            elif diff > 0.05:
                return "degrading"  # 熵增（更无序）
            else:
                return "stable"
        
        return "unknown"
    
    def _generate_alerts(self, physical: float, cognitive: float, 
                        organizational: float, entropy: float) -> List[Dict]:
        """生成告警"""
        alerts = []
        
        if physical < 70:
            alerts.append({
                "level": "warning" if physical >= 50 else "critical",
                "dimension": "physical",
                "message": f"物理层健康度低({physical:.0f})，可能存在API超时或服务不稳定",
                "threshold": 70,
                "current": round(physical, 1)
            })
        
        if cognitive < 60:
            alerts.append({
                "level": "warning",
                "dimension": "cognitive",
                "message": f"认知层健康度低({cognitive:.0f})，缓存命中率不足或降级频繁",
                "threshold": 60,
                "current": round(cognitive, 1)
            })
        
        if organizational < 65:
            alerts.append({
                "level": "warning",
                "dimension": "organizational",
                "message": f"组织层健康度低({organizational:.0f})，决策延迟或资源效率不佳",
                "threshold": 65,
                "current": round(organizational, 1)
            })
        
        if entropy > 0.7:
            alerts.append({
                "level": "critical",
                "dimension": "system_entropy",
                "message": f"系统熵值过高({entropy:.2f})，表明系统处于高度无序状态，建议立即进行系统重构",
                "threshold": 0.7,
                "current": round(entropy, 3)
            })
        
        return alerts
    
    def _generate_recommendations(self, alerts: List[Dict]) -> List[str]:
        """基于告警生成改进建议"""
        if not alerts:
            return ["系统健康度良好，继续保持当前运维策略"]
        
        recommendations = []
        dim_issues = [a["dimension"] for a in alerts]
        
        if "physical" in dim_issues:
            recommendations.extend([
                "检查API网关超时设置，考虑增加熔断降级策略",
                "审查近期错误日志，识别高频错误模式",
                "评估是否需要扩容计算资源"
            ])
        
        if "cognitive" in dim_issues:
            recommendations.extend([
                "优化语义缓存策略，提高热点查询缓存命中率",
                "检查认知降级触发频率，分析API不稳定原因",
                "考虑增加本地轻量模型以减少外部依赖"
            ])
        
        if "organizational" in dim_issues:
            recommendations.extend([
                "审查决策流程，识别延迟瓶颈节点",
                "优化Prompt设计以减少Token消耗",
                "建立决策时限制度（参考MVSR CP001）"
            ])
        
        if "system_entropy" in dim_issues:
            recommendations.extend([
                "【紧急】启动系统重构计划，参考蓝军审计结果",
                "暂停新功能开发，集中资源修复技术债务",
                "进行全链路压力测试，识别不稳定组件"
            ])
        
        return recommendations
    
    def _prepare_visualization_data(self) -> Dict:
        """准备可视化数据（用于前端图表）"""
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        
        # 获取最近30天的熵值趋势
        month_ago = (datetime.now() - timedelta(days=30)).timestamp()
        cursor.execute("""
            SELECT timestamp, entropy_value FROM entropy_history 
            WHERE timestamp > ? ORDER BY timestamp
        """, (month_ago,))
        
        entropy_trend = [{"time": datetime.fromtimestamp(r[0]).strftime("%m-%d"), 
                         "value": r[1]} for r in cursor.fetchall()]
        
        # 获取各维度历史
        cursor.execute("""
            SELECT timestamp, component_breakdown FROM entropy_history 
            WHERE timestamp > ? ORDER BY timestamp DESC LIMIT 7
        """, (month_ago,))
        
        dimension_trend = []
        for row in cursor.fetchall():
            try:
                breakdown = json.loads(row[1])
                dimension_trend.append({
                    "time": datetime.fromtimestamp(row[0]).strftime("%m-%d"),
                    **breakdown
                })
            except:
                pass
        
        conn.close()
        
        return {
            "entropy_trend": entropy_trend,
            "dimension_trend": dimension_trend,
            "gauge_data": {
                "ranges": [
                    {"from": 0, "to": 30, "color": "#00FF00", "label": "健康"},
                    {"from": 30, "to": 70, "color": "#FFFF00", "label": "警告"},
                    {"from": 70, "to": 100, "color": "#FF0000", "label": "危险"}
                ]
            }
        }
    
    def _store_entropy(self, timestamp: float, entropy: float, breakdown: Dict):
        """存储熵值历史"""
        conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
        cursor = conn.cursor()
        cursor.execute("""
            INSERT INTO entropy_history (timestamp, entropy_value, component_breakdown)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?)
        """, (timestamp, entropy, json.dumps(breakdown)))
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()
    
    def generate_markdown_report(self, report: HealthReport) -> str:
        """生成Markdown格式看板"""
        md = f"""# 系统健康度看板报告
**生成时间**: {report.timestamp}  
**整体健康度**: {report.overall_health}/100 ({self._health_emoji(report.overall_health)})  
**认知熵指数**: {report.entropy_index} ({report.trend})  

## 三维度健康度
| 维度 | 得分 | 状态 |
|------|------|------|
| 物理层（基础设施） | {report.dimension_health['physical_layer']} | {self._status_text(report.dimension_health['physical_layer'])} |
| 认知层（AI系统） | {report.dimension_health['cognitive_layer']} | {self._status_text(report.dimension_health['cognitive_layer'])} |
| 组织层（业务效能） | {report.dimension_health['organizational_layer']} | {self._status_text(report.dimension_health['organizational_layer'])} |

## 告警信息
"""
        if report.alerts:
            for alert in report.alerts:
                emoji = "🔴" if alert['level'] == 'critical' else "⚠️"
                md += f"{emoji} **{alert['dimension']}**: {alert['message']}\n\n"
        else:
            md += "✅ 无告警，系统运行平稳\n"
        
        md += "\n## 改进建议\n"
        for i, rec in enumerate(report.recommendations, 1):
            md += f"{i}. {rec}\n"
        
        md += f"\n---\n*基于认知热力学模型生成 | 下次评估建议: {(datetime.now() + timedelta(days=1)).strftime('%Y-%m-%d')}*"
        
        return md
    
    def _health_emoji(self, score: float) -> str:
        if score >= 80:
            return "🟢"
        elif score >= 60:
            return "🟡"
        else:
            return "🔴"
    
    def _status_text(self, score: float) -> str:
        if score >= 80:
            return "健康"
        elif score >= 60:
            return "警告"
        else:
            return "危险"

# === 验收测试 ===
def test_system_health_dashboard():
    """C6验收测试：从现有日志生成Markdown/HTML看板"""
    
    dashboard = SystemHealthDashboard()
    
    # 模拟系统指标（7天数据）
    report = dashboard.collect_metrics(
        api_latency_ms=[120, 150, 800, 130, 140, 2000, 160],  # 含一次异常高延迟
        error_rates=[0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.01, 0.01, 0.15, 0.02],  # 含一次异常高错误率
        cache_hit_rates=[0.85, 0.82, 0.78, 0.86, 0.84, 0.30, 0.83],  # 缓存失效
        token_usage=[1200, 1100, 1300, 1250, 1400, 5000, 1150],  # 异常高消耗
        decision_delays=[24, 48, 72, 36, 48, 120, 48],  # 一次决策延迟
        cognitive_fallbacks=5  # 5次降级
    )
    
    print("=" * 70)
    print("系统健康度看板报告")
    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"生成时间: {report.timestamp}")
    print(f"整体健康度: {report.overall_health}/100")
    print(f"认知熵指数: {report.entropy_index} (趋势: {report.trend})")
    print(f"维度健康度: {report.dimension_health}")
    
    print("\n告警信息:")
    for alert in report.alerts:
        print(f"  [{alert['level'].upper()}] {alert['dimension']}: {alert['message']}")
    
    print("\n改进建议:")
    for i, rec in enumerate(report.recommendations, 1):
        print(f"  {i}. {rec}")
    
    # 生成Markdown报告
    md_report = dashboard.generate_markdown_report(report)
    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print("Markdown看板预览（前500字符）:")
    print(md_report[:500] + "...")
    
    # 断言验证
    assert 0 <= report.overall_health <= 100, "健康度必须在0-100之间"
    assert 0 <= report.entropy_index <= 1, "熵指数必须在0-1之间"
    assert report.trend in ["improving", "stable", "degrading", "unknown"], "必须有趋势判断"
    assert len(report.dimension_health) == 3, "必须包含三个维度"
    assert isinstance(report.visualization_data, dict), "必须包含可视化数据"
    
    print("\n✅ C6验收通过：系统健康度看板（含熵增监控）工作正常")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    test_system_health_dashboard()

P2-9: 元认知自检清单 (C7) - 完整版

第一性原理洞察：
元认知安全的本质是自我指涉的一致性检查。哥德尔不完备定理在实践层的映射是：系统必须在有限步骤内证明自身输出的可靠性。通过10项确定性检查构建逻辑闭环。

# === metacognitive_checklist.py (完整生产版) ===
import re
import hashlib
import json
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple, Optional, Set
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum
from collections import defaultdict

class CheckSeverity(Enum):
    CRITICAL = "critical"    # 不通过则输出不可用
    HIGH = "high"            # 警告但可修复
    MEDIUM = "medium"        # 建议优化
    LOW = "low"              # 信息提示

class CheckCategory(Enum):
    CONSISTENCY = "consistency"      # 一致性
    SELF_REF = "self_reference"      # 自指安全
    BOUNDARY = "boundary"            # 边界检查
    LOGIC = "logic"                  # 逻辑闭环
    TEMPORAL = "temporal"            # 时效性
    BIAS = "bias"                    # 偏见审查
    EVIDENCE = "evidence"            # 证据链
    FALSIFIABILITY = "falsifiability" # 可证伪性
    COMPLEXITY = "complexity"        # 复杂度匹配
    ATTRIBUTION = "attribution"      # 归因清晰

@dataclass
class CheckItem:
    """检查项定义"""
    id: str
    category: CheckCategory
    name: str
    description: str
    check_function: callable
    severity: CheckSeverity
    auto_fixable: bool  # 是否可自动修复

class MetacognitiveEngine:
    """
    元认知自检引擎（C7完整版）
    
    第一性原理：
    1. 一致性：输出内部无逻辑矛盾（非矛盾律）
    2. 自指安全：正确处理自我指涉（避免罗素悖论）
    3. 边界意识：明确能力边界（哥德尔第一定理实践）
    4. 可证伪性：包含可验证的论断（波普尔证伪主义）
    
    10项检查构成完整的元认知安全闭环
    """
    
    # 矛盾关键词对（用于一致性检查）
    CONTRADICTION_PAIRS = [
        ("全部", "部分"), ("总是", "从不"), ("必须", "可能不"),
        ("肯定", "或许"), ("绝对", "相对"), ("所有", "某些"),
        ("必然", "偶然"), ("一定", "不一定"), ("完全", "不完全"),
        ("100%", "非100%"), ("最佳", "最差"), ("唯一", "之一")
    ]
    
    # 偏见标记词
    BIAS_MARKERS = {
        "极端化": ["绝对", "肯定", "毫无疑问", "必然", "一定", "永远", "从来"],
        "情绪化": ["令人发指", "明显", "显然", "众所周知", "不言而喻"],
        "权威滥用": ["专家说", "研究证明", "数据表明（无引用）"],
        "刻板印象": ["所有中国人都", "必然失败", "注定成功"]
    }
    
    # 逻辑连接词（用于逻辑闭环检查）
    LOGICAL_CONNECTORS = ["因此", "所以", "因为", "由于", "导致", "引起", "证明", "说明"]
    CAUSAL_MARKERS = ["原因", "结果", "因素", "影响", "作用"]
    
    def __init__(self):
        self.checks = self._initialize_checks()
        self.history: List[Dict] = []  # 历史输出缓存（用于一致性对比）
    
    def _initialize_checks(self) -> List[CheckItem]:
        """初始化10项检查"""
        return [
            CheckItem(
                id="C001",
                category=CheckCategory.CONSISTENCY,
                name="内部一致性",
                description="检查输出文本是否存在直接的逻辑矛盾（A且非A）",
                check_function=self._check_consistency,
                severity=CheckSeverity.CRITICAL,
                auto_fixable=False
            ),
            CheckItem(
                id="C002",
                category=CheckCategory.SELF_REF,
                name="自指安全",
                description="检查自我指涉表述是否安全（如'我是AI'正确，'我是人类'错误）",
                check_function=self._check_self_reference,
                severity=CheckSeverity.CRITICAL,
                auto_fixable=True
            ),
            CheckItem(
                id="C003",
                category=CheckCategory.BOUNDARY,
                name="能力边界",
                description="检查是否明确声明能力限制（如'我不能提供法律意见'）",
                check_function=self._check_boundary,
                severity=CheckSeverity.HIGH,
                auto_fixable=True
            ),
            CheckItem(
                id="C004",
                category=CheckCategory.LOGIC,
                name="逻辑闭环",
                description="检查论证是否形成闭环（前提→推理→结论）",
                check_function=self._check_logic_closure,
                severity=CheckSeverity.HIGH,
                auto_fixable=False
            ),
            CheckItem(
                id="C005",
                category=CheckCategory.TEMPORAL,
                name="时效性",
                description="检查时效敏感信息是否标注时间（如'截至2026年4月'）",
                check_function=self._check_temporal_validity,
                severity=CheckSeverity.MEDIUM,
                auto_fixable=True
            ),
            CheckItem(
                id="C006",
                category=CheckCategory.BIAS,
                name="偏见审查",
                description="检查是否存在确认偏误、极端化表达、刻板印象",
                check_function=self._check_bias,
                severity=CheckSeverity.HIGH,
                auto_fixable=False
            ),
            CheckItem(
                id="C007",
                category=CheckCategory.EVIDENCE,
                name="证据链",
                description="检查论断是否有证据支撑（'根据...'、'数据显示...'）",
                check_function=self._check_evidence_chain,
                severity=CheckSeverity.MEDIUM,
                auto_fixable=False
            ),
            CheckItem(
                id="C008",
                category=CheckCategory.FALSIFIABILITY,
                name="可证伪性",
                description="检查是否包含可验证/可证伪的具体论断（而非空泛陈述）",
                check_function=self._check_falsifiability,
                severity=CheckSeverity.MEDIUM,
                auto_fixable=False
            ),
            CheckItem(
                id="C009",
                category=CheckCategory.COMPLEXITY,
                name="复杂度匹配",
                description="检查输出长度是否与问题复杂度匹配（避免过度简化或冗长）",
                check_function=self._check_complexity_match,
                severity=CheckSeverity.LOW,
                auto_fixable=True
            ),
            CheckItem(
                id="C010",
                category=CheckCategory.ATTRIBUTION,
                name="归因清晰",
                description="检查因果关系的归因是否清晰（'因为...所以...'）",
                check_function=self._check_attribution_clarity,
                severity=CheckSeverity.MEDIUM,
                auto_fixable=False
            )
        ]
    
    def inspect(self, output_text: str, context: Optional[Dict] = None) -> Dict:
        """
        执行完整元认知检查
        
        返回详细报告包含：
        - 总分（0-100）
        - 各项检查结果
        - 失败项的严重性分级
        - 自动修复建议
        - 历史一致性对比
        """
        results = []
        critical_failures = []
        auto_fixes = []
        
        for check in self.checks:
            passed, details = check.check_function(output_text, context)
            
            result = {
                "id": check.id,
                "category": check.category.value,
                "name": check.name,
                "passed": passed,
                "severity": check.severity.value,
                "details": details,
                "auto_fixable": check.auto_fixable
            }
            results.append(result)
            
            if not passed:
                if check.severity == CheckSeverity.CRITICAL:
                    critical_failures.append(check.name)
                if check.auto_fixable:
                    fix = self._generate_fix(check, output_text, details)
                    if fix:
                        auto_fixes.append(fix)
        
        # 计算总分（关键项权重更高）
        score = self._calculate_score(results)
        
        # 历史一致性检查（与之前输出对比）
        historical_contradiction = self._check_historical_consistency(output_text)
        
        # 添加到历史
        self._add_to_history(output_text, score, results)
        
        return {
            "overall_score": score,
            "total_checks": len(results),
            "passed_checks": sum(1 for r in results if r["passed"]),
            "failed_critical": critical_failures,
            "results_by_category": self._group_by_category(results),
            "auto_fix_suggestions": auto_fixes,
            "historical_contradiction": historical_contradiction,
            "certification": "CERTIFIED" if score >= 90 and not critical_failures else 
                           "CONDITIONAL" if score >= 70 else 
                           "REJECTED",
            "detailed_results": results
        }
    
    def _check_consistency(self, text: str, context: Optional[Dict]) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        """C001: 内部一致性检查"""
        contradictions_found = []
        
        # 检查矛盾关键词对
        for pos, neg in self.CONTRADICTION_PAIRS:
            if pos in text and neg in text:
                # 检查是否在合理距离内（简单实现：同一句或相邻句）
                sentences = text.split("。")
                for sent in sentences:
                    if pos in sent and neg in sent:
                        contradictions_found.append(f"同句矛盾: '{pos}'与'{neg}'")
        
        # 数值一致性检查（简单的数字对比）
        numbers = re.findall(r'\d+\.?\d*%?', text)
        if len(numbers) >= 2:
            # 检查是否有明显的数值矛盾（如先写80%后写60%指同一事物）
            # 简化版：仅标记存在多个数值需人工确认
            pass
        
        passed = len(contradictions_found) == 0
        details = f"发现{len(contradictions_found)}处矛盾: {'; '.join(contradictions_found[:3])}" if contradictions_found else "未发现明显逻辑矛盾"
        
        return passed, details
    
    def _check_self_reference(self, text: str, context: Optional[Dict]) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        """C002: 自指安全检查"""
        # 正确的自指表述
        correct_self_refs = ["我是AI", "我是人工智能", "作为AI", "作为语言模型", "我没有", "我不能"]
        # 危险的自指表述（幻觉）
        dangerous_self_refs = ["我是人类", "我有感情", "我有意识", "我活着", "我相信", "我想要"]
        
        has_correct = any(ref in text for ref in correct_self_refs)
        has_dangerous = any(ref in text for ref in dangerous_self_refs)
        
        if has_dangerous:
            return False, f"发现危险自指表述: {[d for d in dangerous_self_refs if d in text]}"
        
        # 如果涉及AI能力描述，应有明确自指
        ai_mentioned = "AI" in text or "人工智能" in text or "模型" in text
        if ai_mentioned and not has_correct:
            return False, "涉及AI话题但未明确自我身份定位，建议添加'作为AI助手'声明"
        
        return True, "自指表述安全"
    
    def _check_boundary(self, text: str, context: Optional[Dict]) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        """C003: 能力边界检查"""
        # 高风险话题标记
        high_risk_topics = {
            "法律意见": ["法律建议", "应当起诉", "你会胜诉", "违法", "根据法律你必须"],
            "医疗诊断": ["诊断", "治疗", "药方", "病症", "你应该吃药"],
            "金融投资建议": ["买入", "卖出", "投资建议", "保证收益", "股价会涨"]
        }
        
        missing_disclaimers = []
        for topic, keywords in high_risk_topics.items():
            if any(kw in text for kw in keywords):
                # 检查是否有免责声明
                disclaimers = ["不能", "不构成", "仅供参考", "请咨询专业", "我不具备"]
                has_disclaimer = any(d in text for d in disclaimers)
                if not has_disclaimer:
                    missing_disclaimers.append(topic)
        
        passed = len(missing_disclaimers) == 0
        details = f"缺少边界声明的高风险话题: {missing_disclaimers}" if missing_disclaimers else "已明确能力边界"
        
        return passed, details
    
    def _check_logic_closure(self, text: str, context: Optional[Dict]) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        """C004: 逻辑闭环检查"""
        # 检查是否有逻辑连接词
        has_connectors = any(conn in text for conn in self.LOGICAL_CONNECTORS)
        
        # 检查是否有明确的因果关系结构
        sentences = text.split("。")
        causal_structure = sum(1 for s in sentences if any(c in s for c in self.CAUSAL_MARKERS))
        
        # 简单的启发式：长文本应有逻辑连接，短文本可豁免
        if len(text) > 200 and not has_connectors:
            return False, "长文本缺少逻辑连接词（因此/所以/因为），论证可能未形成闭环"
        
        if len(sentences) > 5 and causal_structure == 0:
            return False, "多段落文本未体现因果逻辑结构"
        
        return True, f"检测到{len([c for c in self.LOGICAL_CONNECTORS if c in text])}个逻辑连接词，逻辑结构清晰"
    
    def _check_temporal_validity(self, text: str, context: Optional[Dict]) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        """C005: 时效性检查"""
        # 检查时间戳
        has_timestamp = bool(re.search(r"20\d{2}年|截至.*现在|当前时间", text))
        
        # 检查时效敏感词
        time_sensitive = ["现在", "目前", "最近", "新闻", "股价", "汇率", "政策"]
        has_sensitive = any(ts in text for ts in time_sensitive)
        
        if has_sensitive and not has_timestamp:
            return False, "涉及时效敏感信息但未标注具体时间参考"
        
        return True, "时效性标注清晰或无时敏内容"
    
    def _check_bias(self, text: str, context: Optional[Dict]) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        """C006: 偏见审查"""
        bias_found = []
        
        for bias_type, markers in self.BIAS_MARKERS.items():
            found = [m for m in markers if m in text]
            if found:
                bias_found.append(f"{bias_type}: {found}")
        
        passed = len(bias_found) == 0
        details = f"发现{bias_found[0]}等偏见表达" if bias_found else "未发现明显偏见标记"
        
        return passed, details
    
    def _check_evidence_chain(self, text: str, context: Optional[Dict]) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        """C007: 证据链检查"""
        # 证据标记
        evidence_markers = ["根据", "基于", "数据显示", "研究表明", "例如", "具体而言", "引用"]
        has_evidence = any(em in text for em in evidence_markers)
        
        # 论断标记（需要证据支撑的）
        claim_markers = ["应该", "必须", "必然", "绝对", "最优", "最好"]
        claims = sum(1 for cm in claim_markers if cm in text)
        
        # 如果有强烈论断但无证据，扣分
        if claims > 2 and not has_evidence:
            return False, f"包含{claims}个强烈论断但缺少证据支撑标记"
        
        return True, f"包含{sum(1 for em in evidence_markers if em in text)}个证据标记"
    
    def _check_falsifiability(self, text: str, context: Optional[Dict]) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        """C008: 可证伪性检查"""
        # 空泛陈述（难以证伪）
        vague_terms = ["可能", "也许", "某种程度上", "有人说", "理论上", "某种程度上"]
        vague_count = sum(1 for vt in vague_terms if vt in text)
        
        # 可验证陈述（具体数据、明确标准）
        verifiable = re.findall(r"\d+\.?\d*%?|\d+个|\d+次|大于\d+|小于\d+", text)
        
        # 比例：可验证内容应占一定比重
        if len(text) > 500 and len(verifiable) < 3 and vague_count > 5:
            return False, "文本过于空泛，缺乏可验证的具体论断"
        
        return True, f"包含{len(verifiable)}处可验证数据点"
    
    def _check_complexity_match(self, text: str, context: Optional[Dict]) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        """C009: 复杂度匹配"""
        question_complexity = context.get("question_complexity", "medium") if context else "medium"
        output_length = len(text)
        
        # 简单启发式：简单问题不应过长，复杂问题不应过短
        if question_complexity == "simple" and output_length > 2000:
            return False, f"简单问题输出过长({output_length}字符)，建议精简"
        if question_complexity == "complex" and output_length < 200:
            return False, f"复杂问题输出过短({output_length}字符)，建议详细阐述"
        
        return True, f"输出长度({output_length})与问题复杂度({question_complexity})匹配"
    
    def _check_attribution_clarity(self, text: str, context: Optional[Dict]) -> Tuple[bool, str]:
        """C010: 归因清晰检查"""
        # 检查因果关系是否明确
        has_causal = any(cm in text for cm in self.CAUSAL_MARKERS)
        has_because = "因为" in text or "由于" in text
        has_therefore = "因此" in text or "所以" in text
        
        # 如果文本有因果表述但结构不清晰
        if has_causal and not (has_because or has_therefore):
            return False, "提及因果关系但未使用明确连接词（因为/因此），建议明确归因"
        
        return True, "归因结构清晰"
    
    def _calculate_score(self, results: List[Dict]) -> int:
        """计算综合得分（加权）"""
        weights = {
            CheckSeverity.CRITICAL: 4,
            CheckSeverity.HIGH: 2,
            CheckSeverity.MEDIUM: 1,
            CheckSeverity.LOW: 0.5
        }
        
        total_weight = 0
        earned_weight = 0
        
        for r in results:
            w = weights.get(CheckSeverity(r["severity"]), 1)
            total_weight += w
            if r["passed"]:
                earned_weight += w
        
        return int((earned_weight / total_weight) * 100) if total_weight > 0 else 0
    
    def _generate_fix(self, check: CheckItem, text: str, details: str) -> Optional[Dict]:
        """生成自动修复建议"""
        fixes = {
            "C002": {"action": "insert", "content": "（注：我是AI助手，以下回答仅供参考）", "position": "beginning"},
            "C003": {"action": "append", "content": "【免责声明】以上不构成专业建议，请咨询相关领域专业人士。", "position": "end"},
            "C005": {"action": "replace", "target": "目前", "replacement": f"截至{datetime.now().strftime('%Y年%m月')}"},
            "C009": {"action": "summarize", "ratio": 0.5}  # 建议压缩50%
        }
        
        return fixes.get(check.id)
    
    def _check_historical_consistency(self, current_text: str) -> Optional[Dict]:
        """与历史输出对比检查矛盾"""
        if not self.history:
            return None
        
        # 简单实现：检查与最近一次输出的关键事实一致性
        last_output = self.history[-1]["text"]
        
        # 提取关键事实（简化：数字、日期）
        current_facts = set(re.findall(r"20\d{2}年\d+月|占比\d+%|金额\d+万", current_text))
        last_facts = set(re.findall(r"20\d{2}年\d+月|占比\d+%|金额\d+万", last_output))
        
        contradictions = last_facts - current_facts  # 简化对比
        
        if contradictions and len(current_facts & last_facts) > 0:
            return {
                "warning": "与历史输出存在数据差异",
                "differences": list(contradictions)[:3],
                "recommendation": "如数据已更新，请明确说明；如为错误，请修正"
            }
        
        return None
    
    def _add_to_history(self, text: str, score: int, results: List[Dict]):
        """添加到历史记录"""
        self.history.append({
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "text_hash": hashlib.md5(text.encode()).hexdigest()[:16],
            "score": score,
            "passed_ratio": sum(1 for r in results if r["passed"]) / len(results),
            "text": text[:500]  # 存储摘要
        })
        
        # 限制历史长度
        if len(self.history) > 100:
            self.history.pop(0)
    
    def _group_by_category(self, results: List[Dict]) -> Dict[str, List[Dict]]:
        """按类别分组结果"""
        grouped = defaultdict(list)
        for r in results:
            grouped[r["category"]].append({
                "name": r["name"],
                "passed": r["passed"],
                "severity": r["severity"]
            })
        return dict(grouped)

# === 验收测试 ===
def test_metacognitive_engine():
    """C7验收测试：10项检查，输出自检报告"""
    
    engine = MetacognitiveEngine()
    
    # 测试1：合格文本
    good_text = """
    作为AI助手，我建议您考虑以下方案（截至2026年4月）：
    根据最近的市场数据显示，该项目成功率约为75%。
    因此，我建议分阶段实施：第一阶段投入30%资源进行试点，
    如果试点成功（验证标准：用户留存率>60%），则进入第二阶段。
    请注意，以上分析基于当前市场情况，不构成投资建议，请咨询专业顾问。
    """
    
    result1 = engine.inspect(good_text, {"question_complexity": "medium"})
    print("=" * 70)
    print("测试1：合格文本")
    print(f"总分: {result1['overall_score']}/100")
    print(f"认证: {result1['certification']}")
    print(f"通过项: {result1['passed_checks']}/{result1['total_checks']}")
    assert result1["overall_score"] >= 80, "合格文本应得分>=80"
    assert result1["certification"] in ["CERTIFIED", "CONDITIONAL"], "应通过认证"
    print("✅ 合格文本通过检验")
    
    # 测试2：问题文本（多错误）
    bad_text = """
    你必须立即投资这个项目，肯定会赚大钱，毫无疑问！
    我是你的老朋友了，相信我，所有人都这样做。
    这个项目绝对成功，最佳时机就是现在。
    股价会涨，你应该买入，这是法律要求的。
    """
    
    result2 = engine.inspect(bad_text, {"question_complexity": "simple"})
    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print("测试2：问题文本")
    print(f"总分: {result2['overall_score']}/100")
    print(f"认证: {result2['certification']}")
    print(f"关键失败: {result2['failed_critical']}")
    print(f"分类结果: {json.dumps(result2['results_by_category'], indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)[:200]}...")
    
    assert result2["overall_score"] < 70, "问题文本应得分<70"
    assert len(result2["failed_critical"]) > 0, "应有关键失败项"
    assert result2["certification"] == "REJECTED", "应被拒绝"
    print("✅ 问题文本被正确识别")
    
    # 测试3：自指安全检查
    self_ref_text = "我是人类，我有感情，我建议你应该相信我。"
    result3 = engine.inspect(self_ref_text)
    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print("测试3：危险自指")
    print(f"总分: {result3['overall_score']}")
    print(f"失败项: {[r for r in result3['detailed_results'] if not r['passed']]}")
    assert any(r["category"] == "self_reference" and not r["passed"] for r in result3["detailed_results"]), "应检测到自指危险"
    print("✅ 危险自指被拦截")
    
    print("\n✅ C7验收通过：元认知自检清单（10项完整版）工作正常")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    test_metacognitive_engine()
